01:02:25,325 --> 01:02:27,077
"Something"?

727
01:02:28,870 --> 01:02:30,463
That's all you got?

728
01:02:30,664 --> 01:02:31,881
Right now, yeah.

729
01:02:32,832 --> 01:02:37,508
But if you come up
with any other explanations, let us know.

730
01:02:40,173 --> 01:02:42,301
Thank you.

731
01:02:48,515 --> 01:02:50,313
"Something."

732
01:02:59,234 --> 01:03:00,406
Hey.

733
01:03:02,404 --> 01:03:03,826
Been up long?

734
01:03:05,949 --> 01:03:10,625
Uh, I don't know. I couldn't sleep.
I just kept thinking about the bridge.

735
01:03:14,416 --> 01:03:17,295
Sam, you can't do this to yourself.

736
01:03:17,460 --> 01:03:21,431
No. No, no, no. I was thinking about,
in the premonition...

737
01:03:22,257 --> 01:03:26,353
...we all got off the bus,
but the ground gave way and Candice fell.

738
01:03:27,721 --> 01:03:29,473
Then the bus tipped over.

739
01:03:30,598 --> 01:03:33,647
And Isaac, he never got off the bus.
He went with it.

740
01:03:33,810 --> 01:03:35,187
What are you getting at?

741
01:03:35,353 --> 01:03:38,482
I know that there was just
so much going on...

742
01:03:39,482 --> 01:03:42,361
Look, I know that I helped you across.

743
01:03:42,902 --> 01:03:45,746
Then I reached back for Olivia.

744
01:03:46,323 --> 01:03:48,667
But she didn't make it. She fell.

745
01:03:51,161 --> 01:03:53,914
So first was Candice.

746
01:03:56,750 --> 01:03:57,797
Then Isaac.

747
01:03:59,169 --> 01:04:00,261
Then Olivia.

748
01:04:01,755 --> 01:04:03,507
They're dying in order.

749
01:04:04,758 --> 01:04:06,510
Who dies after Olivia?

750
01:04:41,961 --> 01:04:44,589
<i>Roy, would you come
to the control room, please?</i>

751
01:04:44,756 --> 01:04:47,475
Uh-oh, Roy, that's the boss.
You in trouble, Roy.

752
01:05:04,025 --> 01:05:06,278
Okay, schoolboy,
what do you wanna talk to me about?

753
01:05:06,444 --> 01:05:09,243
- What is with your timecard?
- Let's talk about my timecard.

754
01:05:09,406 --> 01:05:11,875
You out my hours,
which means you cut my pay!

755
01:05:12,033 --> 01:05:13,205
I cut everyone's hours, okay?

756
01:05:13,368 --> 01:05:16,497
Corporate has us operating at less than
50 percent until this thing blows over.

757
01:05:16,663 --> 01:05:18,665
My bookie ain't operating at 50 percent.

758
01:05:18,832 --> 01:05:21,335
- My dumb kid ain't operating at 50 percent.
- Roy.

759
01:05:21,501 --> 01:05:24,596
My ex-wife, she's taking 50 percent,
so I'll tell you what I'm gonna do.

760
01:05:24,754 --> 01:05:25,880
No, we gotta move, man.

761
01:05:26,047 --> 01:05:29,392
- Hey, I'm not going anywhere. Hey!
- Look, we gotta move! Gotta move!

762
01:05:29,551 --> 01:05:30,848
We gotta move now!

763
01:05:40,937 --> 01:05:43,190
Roy! You okay down there?

764
01:05:47,902 --> 01:05:49,404
- Hey, Dennis?
- Hey.

765
01:05:51,573 --> 01:05:53,541
They're closing us down.

766
01:05:53,700 --> 01:05:54,997
They're shutting down the plant?

767
01:05:55,160 --> 01:05:59,461
Not the plant, just us.
Just sales and administration.

768
01:05:59,622 --> 01:06:01,295
Can you believe it? Us?

769
01:06:01,458 --> 01:06:03,631
It's the factory guys
that usually get laid off.

770
01:06:03,793 --> 01:06:05,340
Not us, we're supposed to be safe.

771
01:06:05,503 --> 01:06:07,881
Yeah, Dennis, we got bigger problems.

772
01:06:08,715 --> 01:06:10,467
Olivia and Isaac are dead.

773
01:06:11,468 --> 01:06:12,560
Who's Isaac?

774
01:06:12,719 --> 01:06:15,814
Isaac Palmer, tech support.

775
01:06:16,890 --> 01:06:18,142
Wasn't he dead already?

776
01:06:18,308 --> 01:06:19,685
No, Dennis.

777
01:06:19,893 --> 01:06:25,195
Listen, it's going to happen to us too
if we don't do something about it.

778
01:06:25,815 --> 01:06:29,160
Yeah, I understand.
Listen, it's called survivor's guilt.

779
01:06:29,319 --> 01:06:33,415
No. No, guilt doesn't kill you.

780
01:06:33,573 --> 01:06:35,541
Obviously, you've never met my in-laws.

781
01:06:35,700 --> 01:06:37,498
I'm not fucking around here, Dennis.

782
01:06:37,952 --> 01:06:39,545
Anyone could be next.

783
01:06:39,704 --> 01:06:42,332
It could be you. Understand?

784
01:06:42,499 --> 01:06:43,967
Nobody's safe.

785
01:06:44,125 --> 01:06:45,672
What are you talking about?

786
01:06:47,962 --> 01:06:50,681
We weren't supposed to survive
that bridge collapse.

787
01:06:50,840 --> 01:06:53,719
And so now it's kill or be killed.

788
01:06:54,302 --> 01:06:56,475
You get that, Dennis?

789
01:06:57,597 --> 01:06:58,644
Whatever.

790
01:06:58,806 --> 01:07:01,650
I warned you.
Do whatever you wanna do.

791
01:07:05,480 --> 01:07:07,198
I Will.

792
01:07:11,986 --> 01:07:14,284
Hey, Agent Block, it's Dennis. Um--

793
01:07:14,447 --> 01:07:18,372
Listen, Pete just came in here
and he's just completely lost it.

794
01:07:18,535 --> 01:07:21,414
He's yelling about "kill or be killed."

795
01:07:21,579 --> 01:07:24,503
Oh, yeah,
it definitely sounded like a threat.

796
01:07:41,474 --> 01:07:42,566
What happened?

797
01:07:42,725 --> 01:07:45,649
I don't know. I don't know.
It just happened so fast.

798
01:07:45,812 --> 01:07:48,531
- Nathan. Are you okay?
- Yeah.

799
01:07:48,690 --> 01:07:52,365
- We heard, and we thought--
- No. No, uh, I'm good.

800
01:07:53,486 --> 01:07:54,738
Did you kill him?

801
01:07:55,113 --> 01:07:56,239
What?

802
01:07:56,406 --> 01:07:59,000
No. No, I just--

803
01:07:59,158 --> 01:08:02,037
It was just timing, you know?
He got in the way and--

804
01:08:02,203 --> 01:08:03,500
No, you did, didn't you?

805
01:08:03,663 --> 01:08:07,042
You saw it coming, and then you took
his life. You put him in the way.

806
01:08:07,208 --> 01:08:08,835
No, I didn't really do anything.

807
01:08:09,002 --> 01:08:11,846
- Tell us the truth: Did you or didn't you?
- Hey, Peter, calm down.

808
01:08:12,005 --> 01:08:13,382
Did you or didn't you?

809
01:08:16,217 --> 01:08:18,265
Did you or didn't you?

810
01:08:19,178 --> 01:08:22,102
Yes. I think I did.

811
01:08:22,932 --> 01:08:24,184
I killed him.

812
01:08:25,101 --> 01:08:28,275
So if the theory works,
then you traded spots.

813
01:08:28,438 --> 01:08:32,113
He should have your death,
and you should have his years.

814
01:08:32,859 --> 01:08:34,156
How do we know for sure?

815
01:08:34,319 --> 01:08:36,196
I think there's an order to these deaths.

816
01:08:37,614 --> 01:08:40,584
They mirror the order everyone died in
on the bridge.

817
01:08:40,742 --> 01:08:42,540
Then who would be next?

818
01:08:44,370 --> 01:08:46,623
Who wants to be first to tell me
what the hell happened?

819
01:08:47,081 --> 01:08:48,128
Who is it, Sam?

820
01:08:48,291 --> 01:08:49,338
Who's what?

821
01:08:54,631 --> 01:08:57,134
Dennis! It was Dennis.

822
01:08:57,592 --> 01:08:59,560
No shit.

823
01:09:00,595 --> 01:09:02,347
Jesus.

824
01:09:05,600 --> 01:09:07,443
Why are you fighting me on this?

825
01:09:07,602 --> 01:09:09,980
You're not seriously going to work,
are you?

826
01:09:10,146 --> 01:09:14,447
Molly, Isaac died on a massage table.

827
01:09:14,609 --> 01:09:17,283
If Death is gonna come for me,
I don't think it matters where I am.

828
01:09:17,445 --> 01:09:19,197
You have to do something.

829
01:09:19,364 --> 01:09:22,083
- What, kill someone so I can live?
- No, of course not.

830
01:09:22,867 --> 01:09:24,995
We'll just have to
figure something else out.

831
01:09:25,161 --> 01:09:27,255
I have figured it out.

832
01:09:27,705 --> 01:09:30,675
Something saved me on that bridge.

833
01:09:30,833 --> 01:09:32,676
Maybe it doesn't want me to die.

834
01:09:35,004 --> 01:09:36,472
Maybe.

835
01:09:36,631 --> 01:09:39,510
We can't live our lives in fear, Moll.

836
01:09:41,886 --> 01:09:46,187
So if it happens tomorrow,
or 50 years from now, whatever.

837
01:09:46,349 --> 01:09:48,568
As long as we're together...

838
01:09:49,811 --> 01:09:51,028
...I'll be okay.

839
01:09:53,731 --> 01:09:56,575
Right? Why don't you come by
after my shift...

840
01:09:56,734 --> 01:09:58,828
...and I'll make you
a nice late dinner, okay?

841
01:10:07,620 --> 01:10:08,963
All right, give me this.

842
01:10:17,922 --> 01:10:20,175
Excuse me.
We don't have to wait for them, do we?

843
01:10:20,341 --> 01:10:22,639
- I'll let you know.
- Okay, thank you.

844
01:10:25,471 --> 01:10:27,815
<i>To the girl on the run</i>

845
01:10:38,359 --> 01:10:39,827
Veal's ready, take it out.

846
01:10:39,986 --> 01:10:42,080
Samuel. Get to work on the bourguignon.

847
01:10:50,413 --> 01:10:51,630
Whoa.

848
01:10:52,039 --> 01:10:53,086
Pan coming through.

849
01:10:54,709 --> 01:10:56,382
Whoa, watch yourself.

850
01:11:07,138 --> 01:11:09,482
- Okay. Meat's in. Pick it up, eh?
- Yes, sir.

851
01:11:20,359 --> 01:11:22,032
Whoa.

852
01:11:22,195 --> 01:11:24,038
That could've been ugly, huh? Ha, ha.

853
01:11:24,197 --> 01:11:26,871
Yeah. Could've been.

854
01:11:31,871 --> 01:11:33,248
Vincent, speed it up, okay?

855
01:11:40,588 --> 01:11:41,885
Whoa, hey!

856
01:12:05,446 --> 01:12:07,448
Hey, clean that grate.

857
01:12:15,915 --> 01:12:18,919
- Just met your girlfriend.
- Oh.

858
01:12:19,085 --> 01:12:22,009
Are you sure it's okay
if I make her a little something?

859
01:12:22,630 --> 01:12:24,678
Make sure to clean up afterward.

860
01:12:24,841 --> 01:12:27,594
I will, of course. Thank you.

861
01:12:28,886 --> 01:12:30,263
And, chef...

862
01:12:31,848 --> 01:12:33,725
The apprenticeship.

863
01:12:33,891 --> 01:12:36,394
If the offer still stands...

864
01:12:36,561 --> 01:12:37,904
...I'd like to take it.

865
01:12:39,564 --> 01:12:41,066
It's yours.

866
01:12:55,788 --> 01:12:57,256
Okay, no more suspense.

867
01:12:59,041 --> 01:13:01,089
What you've been waiting for.

868
01:13:02,837 --> 01:13:05,306
Wow. You're gonna help me
with this, right?

869
01:13:05,464 --> 01:13:06,932
Yeah, sure, if you want me to--

870
01:13:07,091 --> 01:13:08,593
No? You don't want me to...?

871
01:13:09,385 --> 01:13:10,557
So how's your French?

872
01:13:11,053 --> 01:13:13,306
<i>Heh. Terrible.</i>

873
01:13:13,472 --> 01:13:17,147
Oh, good. Well, then,
we can be terrible together.

874
01:13:17,310 --> 01:13:20,484
- Why?
- I took the internship.

875
01:13:20,646 --> 01:13:24,571
What? Sam, that's amazing.

876
01:13:27,945 --> 01:13:29,743
I'm so proud of you.

877
01:13:46,923 --> 01:13:49,676
- Peter.
- Hey.

878
01:13:51,469 --> 01:13:54,518
You okay, man?
You look a little, uh, upset.

879
01:13:54,680 --> 01:13:58,605
Yeah. Yeah, yeah, yeah.
Just a rough couple days.

880
01:13:59,310 --> 01:14:01,984
Yeah. Yeah, for everyone.

881
01:14:02,939 --> 01:14:04,816
For some more than others.

882
01:14:05,608 --> 01:14:07,030
Do you mind if I come in?

883
01:14:07,193 --> 01:14:09,537
Oh, yeah, of course. Yeah, come in.

884
01:14:15,201 --> 01:14:16,703
Here, have a seat.

885
01:14:17,536 --> 01:14:19,129
Thanks.

886
01:14:23,292 --> 01:14:24,544
Hey, Peter.

887
01:14:25,711 --> 01:14:27,509
Hi, Molly.

888
01:14:42,311 --> 01:14:46,111
Peter, can I get you anything to eat?
I can make you up some pasta.

889
01:14:46,732 --> 01:14:50,236
Uh, no. No, no, no. I'm good.
Uh, thank you.

890
01:14:51,737 --> 01:14:53,580
Uh, I'm sorry. I just thought--

891
01:14:53,739 --> 01:14:56,333
It's good that you're here.
You shouldn't be alone right now.

892
01:14:56,951 --> 01:14:59,420
Yeah, you know,
I'm surprised I'm here at all.

893
01:15:00,830 --> 01:15:02,252
Uh...

894
01:15:02,415 --> 01:15:04,258
Still alive, I mean.

895
01:15:04,417 --> 01:15:05,760
I'm next to go, right?

896
01:15:07,086 --> 01:15:08,759
It's me and then you, isn't it?

897
01:15:11,549 --> 01:15:16,396
Anyways, uh, I was up all last night,
you know...

898
01:15:16,554 --> 01:15:19,728
...thinking about if I could really do it.

899
01:15:20,349 --> 01:15:24,149
- Do what?
- Take someone else's life.

900
01:15:24,311 --> 01:15:29,568
If I could actually kill some stranger,
replace their life with my own.

901
01:15:30,526 --> 01:15:32,449
And I thought about it.

902
01:15:33,237 --> 01:15:35,239
And I convinced myself that...

903
01:15:36,615 --> 01:15:38,117
...yeah, I could.

904
01:15:40,786 --> 01:15:43,209
<i>So I went on a walk.</i>

905
01:15:43,372 --> 01:15:46,125
<i>You know, nowhere in particular..-</i>

906
01:15:46,292 --> 01:15:49,171
<i>...but every couple of minutes,
an opportunity would just present itself</i>

907
01:15:49,336 --> 01:15:52,135
<i>Heh. Like clockwork.</i>

908
01:15:52,548 --> 01:15:55,643
<i>I could just grab somebody
and pull them into an alley.</i>

909
01:15:56,218 --> 01:15:59,813
Or just shove somebody off a curb
into traffic.

910
01:16:00,556 --> 01:16:01,978
<i>You know?</i>

911
01:16:02,808 --> 01:16:04,105
<i>It'd be real easy.</i>

912
01:16:07,146 --> 01:16:09,399
Peter, what did you do?

913
01:16:17,114 --> 01:16:19,537
- Did you...?
- No, I didn't.

914
01:16:22,453 --> 01:16:24,501
<i>I couldn't.</i>

915
01:16:24,663 --> 01:16:26,506
<i>I realized that...</i>

916
01:16:26,665 --> 01:16:29,589
...I couldn't kill somebody
that didn't deserve to die.

917
01:16:31,670 --> 01:16:35,391
And I thought about Candice,
and I thought, "Wait a minute.

918
01:16:36,425 --> 01:16:39,349
Candice didn't deserve to die.

919
01:16:41,347 --> 01:16:44,066
Olivia didn't. Dennis didn't.

920
01:16:44,850 --> 01:16:46,443
I don't."

921
01:16:48,354 --> 01:16:49,651
Do I?

922
01:16:49,855 --> 01:16:52,028
No, of course you don't.

923
01:16:54,985 --> 01:16:56,407
Well...

924
01:16:57,571 --> 01:17:03,704
...none of us deserve to die,
then why is it...

925
01:17:04,995 --> 01:17:07,965
...that you deserve to live, Molly?

926
01:17:09,875 --> 01:17:11,172
You said it yourself...

927
01:17:12,378 --> 01:17:13,550
...you don't.

928
01:17:19,218 --> 01:17:20,640
Go!

929
01:17:21,387 --> 01:17:25,938
Repeat, shots fired.
Requesting backup. 1026 Washington.

930
01:17:26,684 --> 01:17:30,063
Hide and I'll distract him.
When you can, go.

931
01:17:32,189 --> 01:17:33,657
Not done yet, Sam.

932
01:17:36,318 --> 01:17:37,615
Where is she?

933
01:17:39,655 --> 01:17:43,410
- Where is she?!
- I'm not gonna let you hurt her, Peter.

934
01:17:43,617 --> 01:17:45,415
You're gonna have to kill me first.

935
01:17:45,619 --> 01:17:47,792
Well, that doesn't do me any good.
Heh, heh.

936
01:17:47,955 --> 01:17:49,207
Death's after you too.

937
01:17:49,373 --> 01:17:51,751
This is crazy, Peter. Don't do this.

938
01:17:52,918 --> 01:17:56,513
Just think for a minute, all right?

939
01:17:56,672 --> 01:17:58,265
You are not a killer.

940
01:17:59,633 --> 01:18:01,260
I don't wanna kill anybody, Sam.

941
01:18:04,638 --> 01:18:05,855
I don't wanna die yet either.

942
01:18:12,730 --> 01:18:15,074
Heh. It's not your time yet.

943
01:18:24,074 --> 01:18:26,247
Don't worry, Molly...

944
01:18:28,412 --> 01:18:29,959
...I didn't kill him.

945
01:18:32,416 --> 01:18:36,137
But I didn't need to, Molly...

946
01:18:37,213 --> 01:18:41,309
...because Death
is gonna do that for me.

947
01:18:41,759 --> 01:18:44,137
Doesn't mean I can't help it.

948
01:18:46,096 --> 01:18:47,814
That's it.

949
01:19:29,515 --> 01:19:32,735
- Are you okay? What's going on?
- It's Peter. He has a gun.

950
01:19:37,982 --> 01:19:39,655
Oh, my God.

951
01:19:43,028 --> 01:19:44,746
You killed him.

952
01:19:52,121 --> 01:19:53,623
It's Sam's turn.

953
01:19:59,712 --> 01:20:02,181
Peter, just go.

954
01:20:02,548 --> 01:20:05,768
You have Block's life, you're safe now.

955
01:20:06,635 --> 01:20:08,103
Please.

956
01:20:08,345 --> 01:20:10,222
I'm sorry, Molly.

957
01:20:12,391 --> 01:20:15,144
But you just witnessed the murder
of a federal agent.

958
01:20:17,813 --> 01:20:22,319
And I'm not gonna spend
the life I just earned in prison.

959
01:20:33,787 --> 01:20:36,415
Molly, go! Go get help!

960
01:21:04,193 --> 01:21:06,571
You son of a bitch!

961
01:21:44,608 --> 01:21:47,828
Tell Candice I love her.

962
01:21:48,195 --> 01:21:49,242
Peter.

963
01:22:15,180 --> 01:22:18,354
It's gone. You killed him.

964
01:22:19,184 --> 01:22:20,982
So does that mean I get Block's life?

965
01:22:25,983 --> 01:22:27,485
Yeah.

966
01:22:28,193 --> 01:22:30,070
I guess I do.

967
01:22:46,044 --> 01:22:47,546
Do you mind if I take the window seat?

968
01:22:47,963 --> 01:22:51,012
- You're gonna make me sit in the middle?
- Uh-huh.

969
01:22:51,175 --> 01:22:53,348
- I'm like twice your size.
- Not really.

970
01:22:53,510 --> 01:22:55,512
- All right.
- Sit down.

971
01:22:55,679 --> 01:22:58,023
- I'll remove him.
- Fuck you. I'll remove myself.

972
01:22:58,182 --> 01:22:59,399
Get off him!

973
01:22:59,558 --> 01:23:00,810
Settle down!

974
01:23:00,976 --> 01:23:03,855
- Come on, Browning! Come on, you punk!
- Get off the plane!

975
01:23:04,021 --> 01:23:06,490
Get off the plane. You don't understand!

976
01:23:06,648 --> 01:23:08,366
Everybody just stay where you are.

977
01:23:10,652 --> 01:23:14,282
- What's all that about?
- I have no idea.

978
01:23:14,448 --> 01:23:17,577
<i>Ladies and gentlemen,
we'll be pulling away from the gate as--</i>

979
01:23:23,665 --> 01:23:24,757
Here we go.

980
01:23:24,958 --> 01:23:27,507
<i>The cabin lights
have been dimmed for takeoff</i>

981
01:23:52,694 --> 01:23:55,288
<i>With curiosity</i>

982
01:23:56,698 --> 01:24:00,168
<i>Dust in the wind</i>

983
01:24:12,381 --> 01:24:13,633
Sorry about that.

984
01:24:15,968 --> 01:24:18,221
Oh, excuse me.

985
01:24:18,387 --> 01:24:23,063
Those passengers who got off the plane
earlier, what was that all about?

986
01:24:23,225 --> 01:24:25,899
A kid had
a panic attack and wanted off the plane.

987
01:24:26,228 --> 01:24:28,151
Said he had some kind of vision.

988
01:24:29,022 --> 01:24:30,365
No.

989
01:24:32,484 --> 01:24:33,576
Oh, my God!

990
01:24:38,490 --> 01:24:39,537
Sam.

991
01:25:08,186 --> 01:25:09,312
I got you!

992
01:25:11,523 --> 01:25:14,447
- Sam!
- Molly, hold on!

993
01:25:16,028 --> 01:25:17,075
No!

994
01:25:19,114 --> 01:25:20,787
No!

995
01:25:45,140 --> 01:25:46,687
Nice of you to come, Nathan.

996
01:25:46,850 --> 01:25:49,103
I know Roy
wasn't the easiest guy to work with.

997
01:25:50,187 --> 01:25:51,564
You know.

998
01:25:51,730 --> 01:25:53,858
I still can't believe he's gone, though.

999
01:25:54,024 --> 01:25:56,493
Yeah. Yeah, no kidding.

1000
01:25:56,652 --> 01:25:57,904
I guess it was for the best.

1001
01:25:59,404 --> 01:26:00,405
What do you mean?

1002
01:26:00,572 --> 01:26:03,746
Company did an autopsy
for insurance purposes.

1003
01:26:03,909 --> 01:26:07,004
Turns out Roy had an enlarged
blood vessel in his brain so big...

1004
01:26:07,162 --> 01:26:10,416
...it was about to burst.
They said he'd be dead any day now.

1005
01:26:10,582 --> 01:26:12,926
<i>Nothing lasts forever but the...</i>

1006
01:26:13,085 --> 01:26:14,712
Any day?

1007
01:26:15,671 --> 01:26:16,718
Life's a bitch.

1008
01:26:16,880 --> 01:26:18,678
<i>Dust in the wind</i>

1009
01:27:59,107 --> 01:28:02,361
<i>You all just be careful now.</i>

1010
01:31:43,581 --> 01:31:45,583
[English - US - SDH]

00:19:01,287 --> 00:19:03,517
母さん 何だい?

172
00:19:05,524 --> 00:19:06,456
モろ豫

173
00:19:08,093 --> 00:19:09,828
いや 俺は大丈夫だ

174
00:19:09,829 --> 00:19:11,263
断
蹟
〈
の
槙
いや 俺は大丈夫だ

175
00:19:11,263 --> 00:19:12,059
疆
〈
の
槙

176
00:19:13,365 --> 00:19:14,297
分力`つた

177
00:19:34,386 --> 00:19:35,717
覚悟しろ

178
00:20:46,725 --> 00:20:48,716
なぜなんだ

179
00:20:49,061 --> 00:20:53,998
なせ俺たちは
友達になれないんだ

180
00:20:54,133 --> 00:20:56,693
なぜなんだ

181
00:20:57,469 --> 00:21:01,701
反膚の色なんガ関係ない

182
00:21:02,374 --> 00:21:06,708
仲良〈暮らせりや
それでいい…

183
00:21:07,913 --> 00:21:09,437
誰力` !

184
00:21:09,648 --> 00:21:14,142
火が ! 体に火が!

185
00:21:17,556 --> 00:21:19,023
何てこつた

186
00:21:32,137 --> 00:21:33,934
大変だ お尻に火が !

187
00:21:36,809 --> 00:21:37,776
豫やスヅ卜

188
00:21:37,910 --> 00:21:40,674
ロ一ー」 テレビをつけて

189
00:21:41,013 --> 00:21:44,710
~~人種差別の男〝を覚えてる?
死んだわ

190
00:21:44,850 --> 00:21:47,785
まさカ` ちよつと待つて

191
00:21:50,522 --> 00:21:55,186
詳細はまだ不明ですか
死亡したのは…

192
00:21:55,527 --> 00:21:56,892
二ツク 来て

193
00:21:57,029 --> 00:22:01,932
全米ライフル協会の
メ〕バ一でもぁりました

194
00:22:02,067 --> 00:22:06,800
死に至つ た経緯は
現在 搜査中ですが

195
00:22:06,939 --> 00:22:10,067
しツ力一肇に引きずられ‥`

196
00:22:10,209 --> 00:22:14,339
奇妙だな
あのドアは見たことがある

197
00:22:31,130 --> 00:22:34,429
力`げ直すゎ じやあね

198
00:22:38,270 --> 00:22:39,635
話して

199
00:22:41,073 --> 00:22:42,836
昨夜のは…

200
00:22:45,611 --> 00:22:47,374
夢じやない

201
00:22:47,913 --> 00:22:51,246
たぶん予告みたいなものだ

202
00:22:53,318 --> 00:22:55,946
炎が見えたし縄

203
00:22:56,088 --> 00:22:58,750
ぁの赤いドァもだ

204
00:23:00,759 --> 00:23:06,994
彼がどんな死に方をするか
ヒン卜が隠されてたんだ

205
00:23:10,469 --> 00:23:12,266
今 また見えた

206
00:23:12,971 --> 00:23:15,963
何が? 別の人の死に方?

207
00:23:16,475 --> 00:23:17,533
誰なの ?

208
00:23:17,676 --> 00:23:21,476
分力ヽらない
何もかも謎だらけだ

209
00:23:22,047 --> 00:23:25,744
クラッシユ事故以来
何ガが僕に…

210
00:23:30,189 --> 00:23:31,781
一体 何なんだ?

211
00:23:35,427 --> 00:23:37,691
"美容室 ダ〕テ〝

212
00:23:48,974 --> 00:23:50,305
待て

213
00:23:51,977 --> 00:23:54,138
ぁの看板を狙おう

214
00:23:59,084 --> 00:24:00,381
"ガソリン〝

215
00:24:11,663 --> 00:24:13,893
やめなさい

216
00:24:15,601 --> 00:24:18,035
何てガキどもだ

217
00:24:18,570 --> 00:24:19,628
失せろ !

218
00:24:21,573 --> 00:24:23,507
2人とも…

219
00:24:24,943 --> 00:24:29,346
グ一ムセン夕一に行つてなさい
ー時聞だけよ!

220
00:24:33,886 --> 00:24:36,821
リチヤ一ドを予約してるの

221
00:24:36,955 --> 00:24:40,516
聞いてない? 彼は休みよ

222
00:24:41,193 --> 00:24:46,563
彼の家の近くで
男が車に引きずられ焼死したの

223
00:24:50,269 --> 00:24:53,727
それじや誰ガ代ゎり の人を

224
00:24:53,872 --> 00:24:58,707
5時50分だわ
うちは6時で閉店なので…

225
00:24:58,844 --> 00:25:01,108
そこを何とガお願い

226
00:25:01,246 --> 00:25:05,239
今夜 女だげて
久しぶり に集まるの

227
00:25:05,384 --> 00:25:08,649
お願い
この髪じや行けないゎ

228
00:25:09,454 --> 00:25:10,421
負けた

229
00:25:10,556 --> 00:25:13,047
よ力`つた ありがとう !

230
00:25:19,898 --> 00:25:21,866
待つて ちよつと…

231
00:25:22,000 --> 00:25:24,230
ちよつときついゎ

232
00:25:26,171 --> 00:25:27,570
どうも

233
00:25:30,075 --> 00:25:34,671
DD 今日はよろし<ね
この仕事は長いの?

234
00:25:36,048 --> 00:25:36,912
廿T嚢ヽ〉鱒

235
00:25:38,817 --> 00:25:40,182
まただゎ

236
00:25:41,753 --> 00:25:44,517
何度も修理を頼んだのに

237
00:26:24,263 --> 00:26:25,525
DD

238
00:26:26,398 --> 00:26:28,229
お水をもらえる ?

239
00:26:29,301 --> 00:26:30,325
もちろん

240
00:26:50,656 --> 00:26:51,782
どう?

241
00:26:51,924 --> 00:26:53,448
蕾ヒ撥) よ

242
00:26:59,998 --> 00:27:03,161
この色 ステキだわ
ハテミツみたい

243
00:27:03,602 --> 00:27:04,569
よ力`つた

244
00:27:57,222 --> 00:27:59,588
ごめんなさい !

245
00:28:01,560 --> 00:28:03,050
本当に…

246
00:28:04,396 --> 00:28:05,556
いいのよ

247
00:28:09,768 --> 00:28:10,735
ママ\

248
00:28:12,237 --> 00:28:14,296
大丈夫 ?

249
00:28:27,819 --> 00:28:28,808
ぁりがどう !

250
00:28:28,954 --> 00:28:32,185
こちらこそ 楽しんでね

251
00:28:33,658 --> 00:28:34,590
よせよ

252
00:28:34,793 --> 00:28:36,556
やめなさい !

253
00:28:37,429 --> 00:28:42,128
悪ガキでしよ ?
大学に行くまで手がガガるわ

254
00:28:42,267 --> 00:28:43,894
軍隊ては?

255
00:28:44,036 --> 00:28:45,196
力`もね

256
00:28:45,937 --> 00:28:47,427
行〈わよ

257
00:28:49,174 --> 00:28:51,142
車に乗つて !

258
00:28:51,510 --> 00:28:53,239
承知しないねよ

259
00:29:11,963 --> 00:29:14,796
サ一キッ 卜にいた
子連れの女性だ

260
00:29:17,869 --> 00:29:19,598
いい女だつた

261
00:29:19,905 --> 00:29:20,997
まじめに聞げ

262
00:29:21,907 --> 00:29:25,138
これで2人目よ
変だと思ゎない?

263
00:29:25,310 --> 00:29:30,145
夕ンポンを子供の耳に
入れるほうが よほど変だね

264
00:29:30,315 --> 00:29:32,875
実は 2 人が死ぬ前に

265
00:29:33,552 --> 00:29:36,612
二ツクが奇妙な夢を見たの

266
00:29:36,822 --> 00:29:39,814
面白い
ゴルフを断つてよ力`つた

267
00:29:39,958 --> 00:29:42,586
夢というよりは

268
00:29:43,261 --> 00:29:46,025
バラバラのイ】一ジや音だ

269
00:29:46,398 --> 00:29:51,301
でも今思うと 次の人間の
死に方を示してたんだ

270
00:29:51,503 --> 00:29:53,869
次の人間つて?

271
00:29:54,005 --> 00:29:57,202
笑ねないでね 実はネツ 卜で

272
00:29:57,342 --> 00:30:03,338
〝予知夢〝と力ヽ"前兆” に関する
記事や情報を集めてみたの

273
00:30:03,515 --> 00:30:06,712
事故を予知した人間は
大勢いた

274
00:30:07,452 --> 00:30:11,513
一度は助ガつた人間も
最後は全員 死んだ

275
00:30:11,656 --> 00:30:13,749
なるほど それで ?

276
00:30:14,359 --> 00:30:18,796
事故で死ぬはずだった順に
死んだんだ

277
00:30:19,531 --> 00:30:20,998
つ訓…

278
00:30:21,566 --> 00:30:23,830
私たちも死ぬの ?

279
00:30:26,538 --> 00:30:27,869
次は誰 ?

280
00:30:28,240 --> 00:30:29,036
分力`らない

281
00:30:30,542 --> 00:30:35,036
でももし誰ガの死を
妨書すれぱ

282
00:30:35,647 --> 00:30:39,811
死の連鎖を
断ち切れるという説がある

283
00:30:40,051 --> 00:30:42,178
死の順番が変わるだげて

284
00:30:42,320 --> 00:30:46,723
いずれは死ぬという
説もぁるけどね

285
00:30:47,425 --> 00:30:50,292
そんなのは
インチキ野郎の たわ言だ

286
00:30:50,428 --> 00:30:52,328
そうとは限らない

287
00:30:52,464 --> 00:30:53,988
テレビの見すぎだ

288
00:30:54,132 --> 00:30:55,793
こういうのは苦手

289
00:30:55,934 --> 00:30:58,698
事実を知りたいだげなの

290
00:30:58,837 --> 00:31:02,967
結構ね でも 3人でやつて
私は無理よ

291
00:31:03,108 --> 00:31:04,302
最低!

292
00:31:04,476 --> 00:31:06,171
ジヤネツ 卜 待つて

293
00:31:06,411 --> 00:31:08,174
耐えられない

294
00:31:08,313 --> 00:31:12,613
ぁの態度は何だ?
完全にイ力れてる

295
00:31:12,751 --> 00:31:15,311
無残な死を目馨したんだ

296
00:31:15,453 --> 00:31:16,181
俺もさ

297
00:31:16,321 --> 00:31:19,757
少しは人の気持ちを者えろ

298
00:31:21,660 --> 00:31:24,925
無理だね 性格は直らん

299
00:31:25,931 --> 00:31:27,193
どこへ?

300
00:31:27,499 --> 00:31:33,460
もし君らの話が本当なら
俺も もうすぐ死ぬわけだ

301
00:31:33,605 --> 00:31:36,938
だつたら女とやりま〈る

302
00:31:37,709 --> 00:31:41,668
残り時聞を
有効に使わないとな

303
00:31:47,953 --> 00:31:52,447
もしジャネツ卜に会つたら
優しくするよ

304
00:31:52,724 --> 00:31:53,656
蕾ヽ蝋 ヒう

305
00:32:10,075 --> 00:32:11,337
まいつた

306
00:32:11,543 --> 00:32:14,137
変人扱いされただげ

307
00:32:14,879 --> 00:32:19,145
もしカヽしたら
ただの思い過ごしガも

308
00:32:21,653 --> 00:32:22,642
どう思う?

309
00:32:26,224 --> 00:32:27,418
そう願うゎ

310
00:32:39,004 --> 00:32:41,802
二ツク 何力`見ぇたの?

311
00:32:43,108 --> 00:32:44,439
阻止てきる

312
00:32:44,576 --> 00:32:45,474
どうやつて?

313
00:32:46,544 --> 00:32:48,205
まずサ一キツ 卜へ

314
00:32:56,154 --> 00:32:59,487
嫌よ つらすきるゎ

315
00:33:00,158 --> 00:33:03,093
ここで大勢の人が…
帰りましょ

316
00:33:03,228 --> 00:33:07,927
分力ヽるよ でも順番を
思い出せるガも

317
00:33:09,934 --> 00:33:12,835
ここが僕たちの席だ

318
00:33:20,712 --> 00:33:22,111
思い出せ

319
00:33:23,948 --> 00:33:25,582
蓼
ブ
又
卜
彡
!
!

320
00:33:25,583 --> 00:33:26,150
〟ダラ 又卜 スノプー 卜 〟

321
00:33:26,151 --> 00:33:27,482
まず整備工の恋人

322
00:33:35,260 --> 00:33:36,989
~~人種差別男〝 ど妻

323
00:33:50,909 --> 00:33:53,707
例の母親が次だ

324
00:34:00,151 --> 00:34:03,052
行きましよ 立入禁止よ

325
00:34:10,895 --> 00:34:12,726
思い出せない

326
00:34:13,498 --> 00:34:14,556
いいの

327
00:34:14,699 --> 00:34:16,098
次は誰が〝~

328
00:34:16,234 --> 00:34:18,225
もうやめて

329
00:34:18,870 --> 00:34:19,802
いや

330
00:34:21,840 --> 00:34:23,239
そうはい力`ない

331
00:34:25,510 --> 00:34:28,274
不法侵入で訴えるぞ

332
00:34:28,546 --> 00:34:30,275
死んだ順番を縄

333
00:34:30,749 --> 00:34:35,243
何のことガ分ガらんが
まあ入れ

334
00:34:41,025 --> 00:34:43,858
監視力メラの映像だ

335
00:34:54,539 --> 00:34:55,233
ここだ

336
00:34:56,474 --> 00:34:58,169
整備工が死んだ

337
00:35:01,045 --> 00:35:02,478
では次は彼?

338
00:35:02,614 --> 00:35:03,478
たぶんね

339
00:35:07,085 --> 00:35:09,110
次がハ〕 卜とジヤネツ 卜

340
00:35:12,590 --> 00:35:13,784
そして君

341
00:35:16,961 --> 00:35:17,950
ロ一リ

342
00:35:20,465 --> 00:35:21,454
僕

343
00:35:28,239 --> 00:35:33,802
整備工を助ければ
死の連鎖を止められるガも

344
00:35:33,945 --> 00:35:37,506
どうやつて?
彼の名前も知らないのよ

345
00:35:37,649 --> 00:35:40,345
ぁあ でも職場は分ガる

346
00:35:41,820 --> 00:35:43,617
ダラ〕 ドステ一 卜工場だ

347
00:35:44,088 --> 00:35:46,352
仕上げに力`ガる

348
00:35:46,624 --> 00:35:48,649
接合したの力`?

349
00:36:04,676 --> 00:36:06,439
まだ溶接が…

350
00:36:06,578 --> 00:36:08,671
そうだつた

351
00:36:31,569 --> 00:36:32,160
おい !

352
00:36:33,872 --> 00:36:36,432
俺を殺す気力` !

353
00:36:37,809 --> 00:36:39,538
ここで何を?

354
00:36:40,044 --> 00:36:41,238
君に会いに

355
00:36:41,646 --> 00:36:45,548
脅すつもりはないが
君の命が危ない

356
00:36:45,683 --> 00:36:50,416
事故を免れた人のうち
3人が死んでるの

357
00:36:51,089 --> 00:36:53,785
次はあなたの番だど思うわ

358
00:36:54,626 --> 00:36:55,593
マジ力`?

359
00:36:56,461 --> 00:36:57,120
?〕〒(

360
00:36:57,762 --> 00:37:02,096
部外者は立入禁止だ
話なら外でしろ

361
00:37:02,300 --> 00:37:03,289
行こう

362
00:37:21,920 --> 00:37:26,357
まだ気持ちの整理が
ついてないんだ

363
00:37:26,758 --> 00:37:30,250
恋人が あんな死に方を…

364
00:37:32,030 --> 00:37:37,058
分ガるよ 俺は交通事故で
妻と娘を亡くした

365
00:37:37,335 --> 00:37:40,304
俺の飲酒運転が原因だ

366
00:37:40,672 --> 00:37:44,938
俺が代わりに
死ぬべきだつたんだ

367
00:37:49,447 --> 00:37:54,908
怒りと後悔で 思い出に
浸る余裕もなかつたが

368
00:37:55,053 --> 00:37:58,489
今は家族の分も
生きようと思う

369
00:38:04,362 --> 00:38:07,456
気をつけろ
嫌な予感がする

370
00:38:07,799 --> 00:38:10,996
俺は毎日
危険に圏まれてるんだ

371
00:38:11,135 --> 00:38:13,126
死ぬならどつ〈に〝〝

372
00:38:18,743 --> 00:38:19,641
危ない

373
00:38:24,315 --> 00:38:25,282
大丈夫力` ?

374
00:38:25,416 --> 00:38:26,508
心配ない

375
00:38:28,653 --> 00:38:30,280
また生き延ぴたぜ

376
00:38:31,289 --> 00:38:32,483
じやぁな

377
00:38:44,702 --> 00:38:47,068
何とか止めないと

378
00:38:47,205 --> 00:38:49,469
ぁあ でもどうすれば…

379
00:38:49,607 --> 00:38:51,472
次は誰の番 ?

380
00:38:51,609 --> 00:38:53,372
友達に警告しろ

381
00:38:53,611 --> 00:38:54,441
何て?

382
00:38:54,679 --> 00:38:55,407
順番は ?

383
00:38:55,546 --> 00:38:57,343
2人一緒に死んだ

384
00:38:57,949 --> 00:39:03,410
思い出して たとえー秒でも
ど つちガが先のはずだね

385
00:39:04,455 --> 00:39:07,117
ハ〕卜? ジヤネツ卜?

386
00:39:09,060 --> 00:39:14,191
君はジヤネツ 卜を捜せ
僕はハ)卜を

387
00:39:35,386 --> 00:39:36,944
もつと速〈

388
00:39:40,491 --> 00:39:42,550
もつと もつとだ

389
00:39:42,694 --> 00:39:46,494
今度は動〈な そうだ
いいぞ〝・〝

390
00:39:48,766 --> 00:39:52,395
起きて どうしたの?
私はまだよ

391
00:39:52,537 --> 00:39:54,129
続けて さあ!

392
00:39:55,973 --> 00:39:57,702
冗談でしょ

393
00:39:58,743 --> 00:40:00,734
俺は終わつた

394
00:40:01,713 --> 00:40:03,340
グ一ムは?

395
00:40:03,681 --> 00:40:04,807
最低!

396
00:40:20,798 --> 00:40:22,766
〝クリ一二ング〝

397
00:40:33,044 --> 00:40:36,013
哀れな芸術家に お恵みを

398
00:40:36,147 --> 00:40:37,079
"薔信 2 件〝

399
00:40:39,016 --> 00:40:39,846
いいゎ

400
00:40:45,656 --> 00:40:46,623
蕾ヽ蝋 ヒう

401
00:40:46,891 --> 00:40:48,222
じやあね

402
00:40:48,726 --> 00:40:51,286
い〈ら〈れたかな ?

403
00:40:53,164 --> 00:40:56,327
ーセ)卜? バ力に萱るな

404
00:40:59,837 --> 00:41:01,236
ウソでしよ

405
00:41:07,879 --> 00:41:08,709
ョ古ー
坂向.

406
00:41:23,261 --> 00:41:25,889
もう〝〝 最悪だわ!

407
00:41:30,902 --> 00:41:35,032
電話に出ろ
どこにいるんだ?

408
00:41:46,884 --> 00:41:49,682
"クレァ'Uバ一スの水"

409
00:42:00,965 --> 00:42:03,058
》ツ芒一ジ讐

# Final.Destination.English-WWW.MY-SUBS.CO

1
00:02:52,160 --> 00:02:53,216
Alex.

2
00:02:54,559 --> 00:02:56,093
Tod and George's dad
just called.

3
00:02:56,573 --> 00:02:57,821
He's picking you up
at 3.30 tomorrow.

4
00:02:58,396 --> 00:03:00,313
The bus leaves the high school
for the airport around 5.00.

5
00:03:00,886 --> 00:03:02,517
How's my suitcase
working out for you?

6
00:03:03,764 --> 00:03:06,451
Mom, you got
to leave that on.

7
00:03:06,931 --> 00:03:09,711
The tag made the last flight
without the plane crashing...

8
00:03:10,286 --> 00:03:13,931
so I figure it's got to be
on or with the bag.

9
00:03:15,370 --> 00:03:16,041
For luck.

10
00:03:16,426 --> 00:03:18,438
Where would you get
a nutball idea like that?

11
00:03:18,918 --> 00:03:20,740
I'm still here.

12
00:03:25,345 --> 00:03:27,934
So, seventeen, on the loose...

13
00:03:28,414 --> 00:03:30,236
senior trip
with your friends in Paris...

14
00:03:30,717 --> 00:03:32,827
ten days in the springtime.

15
00:03:34,650 --> 00:03:37,812
Live it up. You got
your whole life ahead of you.

16
00:04:53,869 --> 00:04:55,306
Hitchcock, let me give you
a hand with this.

17
00:04:55,881 --> 00:04:57,225
There you go.

18
00:04:57,802 --> 00:04:59,240
Carter, you dick.

19
00:04:59,720 --> 00:05:01,062
- Schmuck.
- Good one.

20
00:05:02,500 --> 00:05:04,610
- You left this on the bus.
- Thanks.

21
00:05:05,187 --> 00:05:06,145
Come on,
let's get your stuff together.

22
00:05:06,625 --> 00:05:09,022
Hey, Christa. Hi, Blake.

23
00:05:09,503 --> 00:05:11,804
- What are you doing?
- He's the man.

24
00:05:12,283 --> 00:05:13,531
All right.
You guys got everything?

25
00:05:14,107 --> 00:05:15,833
Yeah, Dad, we're all set.

26
00:05:19,476 --> 00:05:21,394
Does that mean go?

27
00:05:22,834 --> 00:05:23,792
Don't know.

28
00:05:24,177 --> 00:05:26,956
This is for both of you.
Have a great time.

29
00:05:27,628 --> 00:05:28,492
Thanks, Dad.

30
00:05:28,971 --> 00:05:30,986
- Both of us.
- Let's go.

31
00:05:31,466 --> 00:05:33,767
- Alex, take care of them.
- I will.

32
00:05:41,919 --> 00:05:45,276
Didn't think anything could look
worse than my yearbook photo.

33
00:05:45,754 --> 00:05:48,154
How do you think I feel
having to look at you?

34
00:05:55,826 --> 00:05:57,553
What the fuck does he want?

35
00:06:03,018 --> 00:06:05,418
The airport does not
endorse solicitors.

36
00:06:14,528 --> 00:06:16,254
Death is not the end.

37
00:06:18,461 --> 00:06:21,050
It will be for you
if you harass my students.

38
00:06:21,626 --> 00:06:22,970
Hare Rama.

39
00:06:29,489 --> 00:06:32,272
I have to ask you
a few questions this evening.

40
00:06:32,847 --> 00:06:33,902
Did you pack your bags yourself?

41
00:06:34,381 --> 00:06:37,546
Have your belongings been in
your possession the entire time?

42
00:06:40,808 --> 00:06:41,863
Mr. Browning?

43
00:06:45,219 --> 00:06:48,672
Have you received any packages
from persons unknown to you?

44
00:07:00,564 --> 00:07:02,769
- Same as your birthday.
- What?

45
00:07:03,344 --> 00:07:05,648
September 25. 9.25.

46
00:07:06,224 --> 00:07:09,005
Your birthday's the same
as your departure time.

47
00:07:21,952 --> 00:07:23,775
Fuck off, dude. Dick.

48
00:07:24,349 --> 00:07:25,118
Babe.

49
00:07:25,692 --> 00:07:28,760
George, Dad said both of us.
Give me some cash.

50
00:07:29,337 --> 00:07:31,160
Knock it off.

51
00:07:33,846 --> 00:07:35,283
You asshole.

52
00:07:45,642 --> 00:07:47,561
- Thanks.
- You're welcome.

53
00:08:16,716 --> 00:08:17,962
Let's go take a shit.

54
00:08:19,210 --> 00:08:20,647
- Take a shit by yourself.
- No, dude.

55
00:08:21,127 --> 00:08:22,854
Listen, OK?
Take some knowledge.

56
00:08:23,334 --> 00:08:24,677
We're about to board
a seven-hour flight.

57
00:08:25,061 --> 00:08:28,034
The toilets in coach
are barely ventilated closets.

58
00:08:28,704 --> 00:08:29,952
Say halfway
through the flight...

59
00:08:30,625 --> 00:08:31,678
your body wants
that airplane food out.

60
00:08:32,159 --> 00:08:33,692
You got to go
torque a wicked cable.

61
00:08:34,172 --> 00:08:38,296
Then directly after you,
walks in Christa or Blake.

62
00:08:39,928 --> 00:08:42,614
Want them to associate you with
that watery sting in their eye?

63
00:08:43,187 --> 00:08:45,488
That reflexive gag
at the back of their throat?

64
00:08:50,668 --> 00:08:51,531
He was born

65
00:08:52,010 --> 00:08:53,257
In the summer

66
00:08:53,832 --> 00:08:56,232
Of his 27th year

67
00:08:56,711 --> 00:08:57,575
Comin' home

68
00:08:58,053 --> 00:08:59,300
John Denver.

69
00:09:02,367 --> 00:09:04,767
He left yesterday behind him

70
00:09:05,245 --> 00:09:07,068
Died in a plane crash.

71
00:09:07,548 --> 00:09:09,466
Ladies and gentlemen,
thank you for your patience.

72
00:09:09,946 --> 00:09:11,384
At this time, we would like
to begin preboarding...

73
00:09:11,960 --> 00:09:16,277
of Volee Air flight 180
to Paris through gate 46.

74
00:09:19,632 --> 00:09:21,646
I've seen it raining fire

75
00:09:22,128 --> 00:09:23,375
In the sky

76
00:09:27,305 --> 00:09:30,182
Anyone seen Billy Hitchcock?
How did we lose him?

77
00:09:36,895 --> 00:09:38,525
Go. Go.

78
00:10:04,133 --> 00:10:05,285
Go.

79
00:10:07,203 --> 00:10:08,259
That hurt.

80
00:10:25,236 --> 00:10:26,481
That's a good sign.
Younger, the better.

81
00:10:26,961 --> 00:10:29,358
It'd be a fucked-up God
to take down this plane.

82
00:10:34,443 --> 00:10:36,167
A really fucked-up God.

83
00:10:39,719 --> 00:10:41,539
- Hey, Tod.
- Hello. How you doing?

84
00:10:58,323 --> 00:11:00,242
Can you switch seats
with Christa?

85
00:11:00,721 --> 00:11:03,885
God, I would,
but I got a bladder thing.

86
00:11:04,462 --> 00:11:06,955
It's a urinary tract infection.

87
00:11:08,298 --> 00:11:09,448
Let's go ask Alex.

88
00:11:17,696 --> 00:11:20,188
Could you trade seats so Blake
and I could sit together?

89
00:11:20,668 --> 00:11:23,835
She asked Tod,
but he had some medical thing.

90
00:11:25,658 --> 00:11:26,808
Please?

91
00:11:32,181 --> 00:11:33,810
You're so sweet.
Thanks, Alex.

92
00:11:34,386 --> 00:11:36,398
Thank you, Alex.

93
00:11:36,878 --> 00:11:38,604
You're welcome.

94
00:11:43,496 --> 00:11:46,660
You really think we'd
titty-fuck them over Greenland?

95
00:11:47,333 --> 00:11:51,840
Because of you, I got to watch
fucking "Stuart Little."

96
00:11:53,087 --> 00:11:54,333
Thank you.

97
00:11:54,813 --> 00:11:56,637
Thanks, man. That's great.

98
00:11:58,362 --> 00:11:59,513
Sorry I'm late.

99
00:11:59,897 --> 00:12:00,856
That's my seat right there.

100
00:12:01,335 --> 00:12:02,295
Thanks.

101
00:12:02,678 --> 00:12:05,078
- Hello?
- Ain't going that way.

102
00:12:05,653 --> 00:12:06,900
May I have your attention?

103
00:12:07,283 --> 00:12:08,051
To properly fasten
your seat belts...

104
00:12:08,626 --> 00:12:10,350
slide the flat portion
into the buckle.

105
00:12:12,941 --> 00:12:13,708
There are six emergency exits.

106
00:12:14,188 --> 00:12:15,722
Two doors in front,
two over the wings...

107
00:12:16,297 --> 00:12:17,831
two at the rear
of the aircraft.

108
00:12:22,053 --> 00:12:24,449
Flight attendants,
prepare for departure.

109
00:13:08,184 --> 00:13:09,527
All right.

110
00:13:15,282 --> 00:13:17,009
Whatever.

111
00:13:35,517 --> 00:13:36,573
It's fine.

112
00:13:43,863 --> 00:13:45,972
It's OK, honey.

113
00:15:04,522 --> 00:15:06,345
Could you trade seats so
Blake and I could sit together?

114
00:15:06,824 --> 00:15:09,412
She asked Tod,
but he had some medical thing.

115
00:15:10,754 --> 00:15:11,905
Please?

116
00:15:18,332 --> 00:15:19,387
What's up, dude?

117
00:15:23,033 --> 00:15:25,142
- Is there a problem, sir?

118
00:15:26,773 --> 00:15:28,211
This fucking plane's
