119
00:15:28,788 --> 00:15:30,323
- Shut up, Browning.
- You're so not funny.

120
00:15:30,898 --> 00:15:33,297
- If this is a joke...
- It's not a joke!

121
00:15:33,968 --> 00:15:34,829
Alex, take it easy.

122
00:15:35,213 --> 00:15:36,842
- Sit down!
- It's going down!

123
00:15:37,417 --> 00:15:39,242
We'll remove you
if this continues.

124
00:15:39,720 --> 00:15:40,967
I'll fucking remove myself!

125
00:15:43,556 --> 00:15:45,858
- That's my seat.
- This plane is going down!

126
00:15:46,338 --> 00:15:47,970
Everybody in the aisle
off the plane.

127
00:15:48,449 --> 00:15:50,749
Just stay where you are.
Sit tight.

128
00:15:51,324 --> 00:15:53,433
This fucking plane
is going down!

129
00:15:54,009 --> 00:15:55,160
Go check him out.
See how he's doing.

130
00:16:06,863 --> 00:16:10,220
- Get the fuck off me!
- Let go, jerk.

131
00:16:10,795 --> 00:16:13,386
You got this?
No one gets back on board.

132
00:16:13,961 --> 00:16:16,071
- That's my call.
- Wait, please!

133
00:16:16,646 --> 00:16:18,755
I've got forty students
going to Paris. Please.

134
00:16:19,235 --> 00:16:20,290
You must understand
my position.

135
00:16:20,673 --> 00:16:22,401
I do, and I apologize
for Alex...

136
00:16:22,975 --> 00:16:24,223
but one of us
needs to be on that plane.

137
00:16:24,703 --> 00:16:27,483
I cannot let those students
go to Paris for ten days...

138
00:16:28,057 --> 00:16:31,127
Sit the fuck down, Carter!
Sit!

139
00:16:31,798 --> 00:16:33,046
One of you
can go on the flight.

140
00:16:33,622 --> 00:16:35,252
Larry.

141
00:16:38,226 --> 00:16:40,240
They're not taking this well.
One of us can get back on.

142
00:16:40,720 --> 00:16:42,827
The rest can take
an 11.10 flight.

143
00:16:43,403 --> 00:16:44,075
I'll stay.

144
00:16:44,458 --> 00:16:46,376
You know the French thing.
Get on the plane.

145
00:16:46,953 --> 00:16:48,104
It's fine.

146
00:16:48,680 --> 00:16:52,131
Hi, I was in the bathroom,
and the lock was stuck...

147
00:16:52,707 --> 00:16:55,007
Wait, I didn't fight
with anyone!

148
00:16:57,023 --> 00:16:58,366
Damn!

149
00:16:58,750 --> 00:16:59,900
Thanks.

150
00:17:04,792 --> 00:17:07,477
I called your parents.
They're on their way.

151
00:17:12,369 --> 00:17:13,521
Stupid Alex.

152
00:17:16,494 --> 00:17:18,698
Alex, talk to me.
Tell me what happened.

153
00:17:19,081 --> 00:17:20,616
I saw it.

154
00:17:21,671 --> 00:17:22,343
I don't know. I saw it.

155
00:17:22,919 --> 00:17:25,413
I saw it on the runway,
I saw it take off...

156
00:17:25,891 --> 00:17:28,002
I saw out my window,
I saw the ground.

157
00:17:28,480 --> 00:17:30,976
And the cabin,
it starts to shake.

158
00:17:31,647 --> 00:17:34,426
The left side blows up
and the whole plane explodes.

159
00:17:35,001 --> 00:17:37,783
And it was so real,
like how everything happens.

160
00:17:38,456 --> 00:17:39,606
Been on a lot of planes
that blew up?

161
00:17:40,086 --> 00:17:41,046
You fell asleep.

162
00:17:41,524 --> 00:17:45,936
We get thrown off the plane
and blow a half day in Paris?

163
00:17:46,511 --> 00:17:47,855
All because Browning
has a bad fucking dream?

164
00:17:48,336 --> 00:17:51,500
Wait! The plane,
it's going to blow up!

165
00:17:52,074 --> 00:17:53,514
Fuck you, Horton.

166
00:17:53,992 --> 00:17:56,105
The only trip you're taking
is to the fucking hospital!

167
00:17:56,679 --> 00:18:00,131
Get off him!
Alex, stop it!

168
00:18:03,297 --> 00:18:05,790
There they go, here we stay.

169
00:18:08,189 --> 00:18:09,242
You're paying for my trip.

170
00:18:09,818 --> 00:18:10,776
I wish you were on that plane!

171
00:18:11,351 --> 00:18:13,654
- You fucking prick!
- Shit!

172
00:18:21,712 --> 00:18:24,013
- Call it in!
- Security!

173
00:19:14,174 --> 00:19:15,803
You're looking at me
as if I caused this.

174
00:19:19,447 --> 00:19:20,886
I didn't cause this.

175
00:19:24,531 --> 00:19:25,874
Are there any survivors?

176
00:19:31,821 --> 00:19:32,876
How should I know?

177
00:19:36,520 --> 00:19:38,919
- You think I'm some...
- He's not a witch.

178
00:19:45,247 --> 00:19:46,303
I'm Howard Seigel...

179
00:19:46,783 --> 00:19:48,126
National Transportation
and Safety Board.

180
00:19:48,701 --> 00:19:50,141
We've contacted
all your families.

181
00:19:50,619 --> 00:19:51,385
They are on the way.

182
00:19:51,769 --> 00:19:55,029
Is there anyone here
who feels they might need...

183
00:19:55,605 --> 00:19:58,770
medical attention
or spiritual counseling?

184
00:19:59,347 --> 00:20:02,224
What's going on?
Are there any survivors?

185
00:20:03,951 --> 00:20:06,541
The cause for the explosion
is still undetermined.

186
00:20:07,116 --> 00:20:08,935
The Nassau County
authorities are on the scene...

187
00:20:09,416 --> 00:20:11,909
naval search and rescue
en route.

188
00:20:12,293 --> 00:20:13,925
Excuse me. I'm Agent Weine.

189
00:20:14,500 --> 00:20:16,993
This is Agent Schreck.
We're with the FBI.

190
00:20:17,472 --> 00:20:19,390
I understand how
you all must be feeling...

191
00:20:19,870 --> 00:20:20,829
and I know it's going
to be difficult...

192
00:20:21,309 --> 00:20:23,515
but we need to ask questions
regarding today's events...

193
00:20:23,995 --> 00:20:25,625
while they're still fresh
in your mind.

194
00:20:26,104 --> 00:20:27,639
This will prove invaluable
to our rescue attempts...

195
00:20:28,118 --> 00:20:30,613
and any potential
criminal investigation.

196
00:20:34,545 --> 00:20:35,216
You said, "Listen to me.

197
00:20:35,696 --> 00:20:37,806
"This plane will explode
on takeoff."

198
00:20:38,286 --> 00:20:40,493
How did you know that?

199
00:20:41,164 --> 00:20:42,315
I got this feeling.

200
00:20:44,519 --> 00:20:45,286
This weird feeling.

201
00:20:45,765 --> 00:20:47,684
Did you take any sedatives
before boarding?

202
00:20:48,164 --> 00:20:49,602
On board, did you take
any sleeping pills...

203
00:20:49,986 --> 00:20:51,041
narcotics, hallucinogens?

204
00:20:51,615 --> 00:20:53,534
Do you take any drugs?

205
00:20:55,549 --> 00:20:56,893
I saw it.

206
00:20:59,767 --> 00:21:00,726
I saw it happen.

207
00:21:01,110 --> 00:21:02,357
I saw the plane explode.

208
00:21:02,932 --> 00:21:04,181
Did the weird feelings
have anything to do...

209
00:21:04,756 --> 00:21:07,826
with your saying you wished
Carter Horton was on it...

210
00:21:08,401 --> 00:21:09,744
just before it exploded?

211
00:21:11,852 --> 00:21:13,484
Then why'd you say it?

212
00:21:14,443 --> 00:21:16,266
I didn't really think
it was going to happen.

213
00:21:16,841 --> 00:21:17,799
If that's the case, Alex...

214
00:21:18,375 --> 00:21:21,254
why did you really
get off the plane?

215
00:21:24,035 --> 00:21:25,952
My brother George...

216
00:21:26,527 --> 00:21:29,404
he told me to go
keep an eye on Alex.

217
00:21:30,652 --> 00:21:34,007
And so he stayed and...

218
00:21:39,954 --> 00:21:42,448
He told me
to get off the plane.

219
00:21:42,928 --> 00:21:45,230
Larry Murnau said
I should get back on...

220
00:21:45,709 --> 00:21:48,587
but I told him
that he should go.

221
00:21:50,794 --> 00:21:52,517
I sent him back on the plane.

222
00:21:55,588 --> 00:21:57,315
All right.

223
00:21:57,794 --> 00:21:59,711
Nobody forced you off the plane.

224
00:22:00,191 --> 00:22:02,684
You said you weren't friends
with those that were...

225
00:22:03,259 --> 00:22:05,275
so, Clear, why did you
get off the plane?

226
00:22:07,480 --> 00:22:09,782
Because I saw
and I heard Alex...

227
00:22:14,578 --> 00:22:15,825
and I believed him.

228
00:22:37,405 --> 00:22:38,556
There he is.

229
00:23:04,069 --> 00:23:05,889
Thank you for the ride.

230
00:24:06,121 --> 00:24:07,657
You are looking
at the first piece of debris...

231
00:24:08,230 --> 00:24:10,627
to wash ashore
from Volee Air flight 180...

232
00:24:11,106 --> 00:24:13,793
which erupted shortly after
a 9.25 p.m. Takeoff...

233
00:24:14,369 --> 00:24:16,287
from John F. Kennedy Airport
in New York.

234
00:24:16,766 --> 00:24:18,396
There are no known survivors.

235
00:24:18,972 --> 00:24:21,083
Coast Guard and naval
search and rescue efforts...

236
00:24:21,563 --> 00:24:22,808
are continuing
at this moment...

237
00:24:23,385 --> 00:24:25,875
even as jet fuel burns
on the ocean's surface.

238
00:24:26,355 --> 00:24:28,465
Authorities are pessimistic
about the possibility...

239
00:24:28,945 --> 00:24:31,440
of finding anyone alive
from the ill-fated flight.

240
00:24:31,920 --> 00:24:35,277
All 287 passengers
are feared dead.

241
00:24:35,947 --> 00:24:36,619
Amongst the travelers...

242
00:24:37,002 --> 00:24:39,210
a class of forty students
and four teachers...

243
00:24:39,785 --> 00:24:42,279
from Mount Abraham High School
in south-east New York...

244
00:24:42,757 --> 00:24:44,388
en route to Paris
on a field trip.

245
00:24:44,963 --> 00:24:46,500
There are reports
several students...

246
00:24:46,979 --> 00:24:49,664
were removed from the aircraft
moments before departure...

247
00:24:50,143 --> 00:24:51,677
however, investigators
remain guarded...

248
00:24:52,252 --> 00:24:53,595
about the specifics
of this incident.

249
00:24:54,171 --> 00:24:57,145
Authorities from National
Transportation and Safety...

250
00:24:57,720 --> 00:24:58,584
and the Federal Bureau
of Investigation...

251
00:24:59,063 --> 00:25:01,746
have arrived at JFK to begin
the initial investigation.

252
00:25:02,225 --> 00:25:05,296
Eyewitnesses at the airport,
as well as on Long Island...

253
00:25:05,872 --> 00:25:08,173
report seeing the plane explode.

254
00:25:08,654 --> 00:25:10,667
No other aircraft were
visibly near flight 180.

255
00:25:11,147 --> 00:25:12,394
At JFK,
air traffic controllers...

256
00:25:12,874 --> 00:25:15,271
are corroborating
these eyewitness accounts.

257
00:25:15,751 --> 00:25:18,147
After the explosion,
debris apparently rained...

258
00:25:18,626 --> 00:25:20,930
into the Atlantic Ocean
for several miles.

259
00:25:21,601 --> 00:25:24,958
Investigators are cautious about
verifying these accounts...

260
00:25:25,436 --> 00:25:27,258
believing they'll learn more...

261
00:25:46,922 --> 00:25:48,840
Thirty-nine days
have passed...

262
00:25:49,416 --> 00:25:51,333
since we lost
our thirty-nine loved ones...

263
00:25:51,812 --> 00:25:53,346
friends, and teachers.

264
00:25:54,593 --> 00:25:55,649
As each day passes...

265
00:25:56,128 --> 00:25:58,622
without a determining cause
for the accident...

266
00:25:59,006 --> 00:26:01,019
we ask ourselves, "Why?"

267
00:26:02,553 --> 00:26:04,569
"Ecclesiastes" tells us...

268
00:26:05,144 --> 00:26:07,542
"Man no more knows
his own time...

269
00:26:08,115 --> 00:26:10,513
"than fish taken
in the fatal net...

270
00:26:11,088 --> 00:26:13,007
"or birds trapped in the snare.

271
00:26:13,583 --> 00:26:16,461
"Like these,
the children of men caught...

272
00:26:17,036 --> 00:26:19,050
"when the time falls
suddenly upon them."

273
00:26:19,625 --> 00:26:22,312
And so,
before we can heal...

274
00:26:22,887 --> 00:26:26,532
before we escape the presence
of death and time...

275
00:26:27,107 --> 00:26:29,601
we must mourn
and celebrate theirs...

276
00:26:30,081 --> 00:26:31,998
with this memorial.

277
00:27:14,774 --> 00:27:16,979
I hope you don't think because
my name ain't on this wall...

278
00:27:17,457 --> 00:27:19,472
that I owe you anything.

279
00:27:19,952 --> 00:27:20,913
I don't.

280
00:27:21,296 --> 00:27:23,980
All I owe these people is
to live my life to the fullest.

281
00:27:24,556 --> 00:27:27,146
Why don't you
stay off the J.D., then?

282
00:27:27,721 --> 00:27:29,833
Don't you ever fucking
tell me what to do.

283
00:27:30,312 --> 00:27:32,039
I control my life, not you.

284
00:27:35,969 --> 00:27:37,695
I'm never going to die.

285
00:27:49,876 --> 00:27:51,505
I took my driver's test
last week at the DMV.

286
00:27:51,985 --> 00:27:52,847
Got a seventy.

287
00:27:53,232 --> 00:27:54,767
It's the lowest score
you can get, but I passed.

288
00:27:55,247 --> 00:27:56,973
The thing is,
when I was done with the test...

289
00:27:57,453 --> 00:27:59,563
you know the guy that drives
with you during the test?

290
00:28:00,042 --> 00:28:00,810
Anyways, he goes...

291
00:28:01,288 --> 00:28:04,839
"Young man, you're going to die
at a very young age."

292
00:28:08,482 --> 00:28:09,154
Is that true?

293
00:28:09,538 --> 00:28:12,416
Not now, not here,
not ever, Billy.

294
00:28:16,060 --> 00:28:18,265
If I ask out Cynthia Paster,
will she say no?

295
00:28:18,744 --> 00:28:19,992
Fuck.

296
00:28:30,637 --> 00:28:33,131
Don't talk to me.
You scare the hell out of me.

297
00:28:34,570 --> 00:28:36,297
I'm going up.

298
00:28:48,861 --> 00:28:50,300
I don't want you
to take this the wrong way.

299
00:28:50,778 --> 00:28:54,038
I miss you, you know?

300
00:28:55,669 --> 00:28:57,396
Yeah, I miss you, too.

301
00:28:57,972 --> 00:29:00,753
But my father,
he doesn't understand.

302
00:29:03,535 --> 00:29:05,356
Look, when he gets over
this thing...

303
00:29:05,932 --> 00:29:08,522
you and me,
we'll road trip to the city...

304
00:29:09,097 --> 00:29:10,631
catch the Yanks.

305
00:29:11,879 --> 00:29:13,703
We'll do that.

306
00:29:14,182 --> 00:29:15,237
Cool.

307
00:29:17,537 --> 00:29:19,070
I better go.

308
00:29:19,551 --> 00:29:20,990
This thing Ms Lewton
showed me in class...

309
00:29:21,373 --> 00:29:23,291
they're going to let me read it.

310
00:29:23,771 --> 00:29:25,689
It says what I'm feeling.

311
00:29:27,894 --> 00:29:29,047
Take care, man.

312
00:29:38,829 --> 00:29:40,555
Because of you,
I'm still alive.

313
00:29:43,337 --> 00:29:44,487
Thank you.

314
00:29:51,488 --> 00:29:55,039
We say that the hour of death
cannot be forecast.

315
00:29:56,669 --> 00:29:59,353
But when we say this...

316
00:29:59,832 --> 00:30:01,368
we imagine
that the hour is placed...

317
00:30:01,942 --> 00:30:04,821
in an obscure
and distant future.

318
00:30:05,397 --> 00:30:07,792
It never occurs to us
that it has any connection...

319
00:30:08,368 --> 00:30:10,669
with the day already begun...

320
00:30:11,149 --> 00:30:14,699
or that death could arrive
this same afternoon.

321
00:30:15,371 --> 00:30:18,248
This afternoon
which is so certain...

322
00:30:18,727 --> 00:30:23,906
and which has every hour
filled in advance.

323
00:35:54,314 --> 00:35:55,464
What happened?

324
00:35:56,327 --> 00:35:57,669
Where's Tod?

325
00:36:03,905 --> 00:36:05,057
Get out of here.

326
00:36:18,673 --> 00:36:21,073
- Didn't you see it?
- What happened?

327
00:36:21,648 --> 00:36:25,578
You caused Tod
so much guilt over George...

328
00:36:26,153 --> 00:36:27,783
he took his own life.

329
00:36:31,334 --> 00:36:33,635
He wouldn't do that.

330
00:36:34,116 --> 00:36:37,281
He said we'd be friends again
after you got better.

331
00:36:37,856 --> 00:36:40,638
Why would he make plans
if he was going to kill himself?

332
00:36:41,117 --> 00:36:43,131
Mr. Waggner.

333
00:37:38,759 --> 00:37:39,429
Almost autumn.

334
00:37:40,005 --> 00:37:42,594
It's only the end of June.

335
00:37:44,896 --> 00:37:46,336
Yeah, but everything's
always in transition.

336
00:37:46,911 --> 00:37:48,638
If you focus, even now,
just one week into summer...

337
00:37:49,214 --> 00:37:51,034
you can almost feel
autumn coming.

338
00:37:51,609 --> 00:37:53,240
I like being able
to see the future.

339
00:37:53,815 --> 00:37:57,077
Why were you at
Tod's house last night?

340
00:38:00,240 --> 00:38:01,583
I've seen enough TV to know...

341
00:38:02,064 --> 00:38:03,887
the FBI doesn't investigate
teen suicides.

342
00:38:04,367 --> 00:38:06,284
They were there last night.
That means, one...

343
00:38:06,763 --> 00:38:07,913
they still don't have a clue
what caused the crash.

344
00:38:08,392 --> 00:38:09,447
Two... they haven't
ruled out anything...

345
00:38:09,927 --> 00:38:11,366
and the fact seven people
got off the plane...

346
00:38:11,846 --> 00:38:12,806
is probably weird enough.

347
00:38:13,189 --> 00:38:15,011
Not to mention, one of
those people had a vision...

348
00:38:15,492 --> 00:38:17,217
of the plane exploding...

349
00:38:17,696 --> 00:38:20,383
minutes before it actually did
is highly suspicious.

350
00:38:21,055 --> 00:38:22,396
And it doesn't help that
the visionary's friend...

351
00:38:22,877 --> 00:38:24,219
just committed suicide.

352
00:38:25,657 --> 00:38:27,767
Why were you there last night?

353
00:38:33,617 --> 00:38:35,248
You know what this is?

354
00:38:37,935 --> 00:38:39,373
This is a...

355
00:38:42,345 --> 00:38:44,166
This is Springy Head Guy.

356
00:38:45,221 --> 00:38:49,539
It's you. Not a likeness.

357
00:38:50,114 --> 00:38:52,896
It's how
you make me feel, Alex.

358
00:38:54,240 --> 00:38:56,062
I'm sorry.

359
00:38:56,636 --> 00:38:58,265
No. Like you, the sculpture
doesn't even know...

360
00:38:58,745 --> 00:39:00,566
what or why it is.

361
00:39:01,047 --> 00:39:02,870
It's reluctant to take form...

362
00:39:03,350 --> 00:39:05,076
and yet creating an absolute...

363
00:39:05,556 --> 00:39:08,145
but incomprehensible
attraction.

364
00:39:10,254 --> 00:39:11,406
In four years
of high school...

365
00:39:11,981 --> 00:39:13,516
we haven't said
one word to each other.

366
00:39:13,900 --> 00:39:16,584
At that moment, on the plane,
I felt what you felt.

367
00:39:17,256 --> 00:39:19,270
I didn't even know where
those emotions came from...

368
00:39:19,847 --> 00:39:21,670
until you started
freaking out.

369
00:39:22,244 --> 00:39:26,080
I didn't see what you saw,
but I felt it.

370
00:39:26,560 --> 00:39:28,767
You can still feel it,
can't you?

371
00:39:29,342 --> 00:39:32,412
Something from that day
is still with you.

372
00:39:34,807 --> 00:39:37,014
I know because
I can still feel you.

373
00:39:39,699 --> 00:39:41,712
That's why I was there
last night.

374
00:39:44,686 --> 00:39:46,606
I've never dealt
with death before.

375
00:39:47,085 --> 00:39:49,387
This could all be in our head.

376
00:39:50,535 --> 00:39:53,029
It just feels like
it's all around us.

377
00:39:53,605 --> 00:39:54,852
It?

378
00:39:57,442 --> 00:39:59,840
What if Tod was just the first?

379
00:40:01,182 --> 00:40:02,045
Of us.

380
00:40:02,526 --> 00:40:04,540
Is that something
that you're feeling?

381
00:40:06,362 --> 00:40:07,129
I don't know.

382
00:40:07,704 --> 00:40:10,677
I wish I could see him
one last time.

383
00:40:11,159 --> 00:40:13,077
Maybe I would know.

384
00:40:13,651 --> 00:40:15,280
Then let's go see him.

385
00:40:44,532 --> 00:40:45,781
Easy.

386
00:40:48,754 --> 00:40:50,288
Gives me a rush.

387
00:40:52,302 --> 00:40:54,221
This place?

388
00:40:54,700 --> 00:40:56,715
Doing something
I'm not supposed to.

389
00:41:00,071 --> 00:41:01,125
Come on.

390
00:41:06,209 --> 00:41:07,647
Hurry up.

391
00:41:21,267 --> 00:41:23,570
- Is that him?
- I think.

392
00:41:24,240 --> 00:41:26,063
Why did they make him up
like Michael Jackson?

393
00:41:26,637 --> 00:41:28,652
That's him.

394
00:41:29,228 --> 00:41:32,774
Whatever it was that made him
Tod is definitely gone.

395
00:41:35,556 --> 00:41:36,804
Holy Christ!

396
00:41:39,681 --> 00:41:40,833
Please.

397
00:41:42,367 --> 00:41:43,805
You'll wake the dead.

398
00:41:54,261 --> 00:41:55,315
Why'd his hand do that?

399
00:41:55,796 --> 00:41:56,657
Chemicals.

400
00:41:57,233 --> 00:42:00,589
The vascular flush
creates cadaveric spasms.

401
00:42:01,261 --> 00:42:04,427
Look, I'm his friend...

402
00:42:04,907 --> 00:42:06,345
I know who you are.

403
00:42:07,878 --> 00:42:09,316
What are all those tiny marks?

404
00:42:09,795 --> 00:42:12,865
Cuticle lacerations
from pulling at the wire.

405
00:42:13,537 --> 00:42:15,072
Pulling at the wire?

406
00:42:15,647 --> 00:42:17,757
If he was pulling at the wire,
he didn't kill himself.

407
00:42:18,237 --> 00:42:19,100
It was an accident.

408
00:42:19,581 --> 00:42:22,074
In death,
there are no accidents...

409
00:42:22,554 --> 00:42:25,046
no coincidences, no mishaps...

410
00:42:25,525 --> 00:42:27,251
and no escapes.

411
00:42:29,171 --> 00:42:32,144
What you have to realize
is we're all just a mouse...

412
00:42:32,718 --> 00:42:34,061
a cat has by the tail.

413
00:42:34,636 --> 00:42:36,844
Every single move we make...

414
00:42:37,322 --> 00:42:38,858
from the mundane
to the monumental...

415
00:42:39,433 --> 00:42:41,829
the red light
that we stop at or run...

416
00:42:42,310 --> 00:42:44,803
the people we have sex with
or won't with us...

417
00:42:45,476 --> 00:42:47,872
airplanes that we ride
or walk out of...

418
00:42:48,352 --> 00:42:51,037
it's all part of
death's sadistic design...

419
00:42:51,518 --> 00:42:53,052
leading to the grave.

420
00:42:54,780 --> 00:42:56,218
Design.

421
00:42:56,794 --> 00:42:58,806
Does that mean if...

422
00:42:59,286 --> 00:43:01,011
you figure out the design,
you can cheat death?

423
00:43:01,587 --> 00:43:04,657
Alex, you've
already done that...

424
00:43:05,233 --> 00:43:06,670
by walking off the plane.

425
00:43:07,150 --> 00:43:08,877
Your friend's departure...

426
00:43:09,356 --> 00:43:13,193
shows that death has
a new design for all of you.

427
00:43:13,768 --> 00:43:15,685
Now you have to figure out...

428
00:43:16,165 --> 00:43:19,619
how and when
it's coming back at you.

429
00:43:21,057 --> 00:43:21,920
Play your hunch...

430
00:43:22,496 --> 00:43:24,894
if you think
you can get away with it.

431
00:43:25,374 --> 00:43:27,099
But remember...

432
00:43:27,674 --> 00:43:30,073
the risk of cheating the plan...

433
00:43:30,649 --> 00:43:32,374
of disrespecting the design...

434
00:43:32,950 --> 00:43:37,555
could incite a fury to terrorize
even the Grim Reaper.

435
00:43:39,185 --> 00:43:43,021
And you don't even want to
fuck with that Mack Daddy.

436
00:43:47,338 --> 00:43:48,776
OK, then. Well...

437
00:43:49,256 --> 00:43:51,843
I'm sorry we broke in,
and...

438
00:43:52,514 --> 00:43:55,105
No harm, no foul.

439
00:43:59,229 --> 00:44:00,476
I'll see you soon.

440
00:44:06,996 --> 00:44:09,395
The mortician said
that death has a design.

441
00:44:09,874 --> 00:44:13,329
I'm talking about omens.

442
00:44:13,904 --> 00:44:15,821
How do we know,
that just by sitting here...

443
00:44:16,301 --> 00:44:18,506
by sipping this coffee
or breathing the air...

444
00:44:18,984 --> 00:44:20,425
or even crossing
the intersection...

445
00:44:21,001 --> 00:44:22,631
we haven't started in motion...

446
00:44:23,111 --> 00:44:24,644
the events that will
some day lead to our death...

447
00:44:25,220 --> 00:44:27,426
forty years from now,
ten years, tomorrow?

448
00:44:29,058 --> 00:44:31,647
We don't,
unless we open ourselves up...

449
00:44:32,125 --> 00:44:34,715
to the signs
it's willing to show us.

450
00:44:38,266 --> 00:44:39,512
I don't understand.

451
00:44:39,896 --> 00:44:42,388
Did you see Tod die?

452
00:44:43,059 --> 00:44:45,266
Did it happen again,
like on the plane?

453
00:44:45,745 --> 00:44:48,430
No, it didn't,
but it might as well have.

454
00:44:48,911 --> 00:44:52,075
This is a message
from something, Clear...

455
00:44:52,650 --> 00:44:55,145
or someone,
hinting at a design.

456
00:44:55,624 --> 00:44:56,870
Total bullshit.

457
00:44:57,351 --> 00:44:59,267
You can find death omens
anywhere you want to.

458
00:44:59,842 --> 00:45:02,337
Coffee starts with a "C"
and ends with an "E".

459
00:45:02,912 --> 00:45:05,310
So does "choke."
We'll choke to death?

460
00:45:05,790 --> 00:45:07,420
I want to hear you...

461
00:45:07,996 --> 00:45:09,913
but we'll go nuts
if you start with this shit.

462
00:45:10,585 --> 00:45:14,135
The mortician said
that death has a design, right?

463
00:45:14,518 --> 00:45:17,778
Now, what if
you, me, Tod, Carter...

464
00:45:18,450 --> 00:45:21,041
Terry, Billy, Mrs. Lewton
messed up that design?

465
00:45:21,520 --> 00:45:23,532
For whatever reason,
I saw death's plan...

466
00:45:24,109 --> 00:45:25,834
and we cheated it.

467
00:45:26,314 --> 00:45:27,752
But what if it was our time?

468
00:45:28,330 --> 00:45:29,673
What if we were not meant
to get off that plane?

469
00:45:30,152 --> 00:45:32,836
What if it still is our time?

470
00:45:33,315 --> 00:45:36,287
If it is,
then it's not finished...

471
00:45:36,863 --> 00:45:40,221
and we will die.
Now, not later.

472
00:45:40,796 --> 00:45:46,169
Unless we find the patterns
and cheat it again.

473
00:45:55,471 --> 00:45:57,868
After hearing you,
I do believe...

474
00:45:58,923 --> 00:46:01,032
that Tod killed himself.

475
00:46:32,971 --> 00:46:34,314
Baby, come on, not now.

476
00:46:37,385 --> 00:46:38,727
Looks like we have
a reunion here.

477
00:46:39,205 --> 00:46:39,973
Let it go.

478
00:46:40,357 --> 00:46:42,081
- When are you moving?
- Couple weeks.

479
00:46:42,561 --> 00:46:44,191
- That's too bad.
- Carter, you dick!

480
00:46:44,671 --> 00:46:46,207
Losing our favorite teacher.

481
00:46:46,782 --> 00:46:49,181
Guys, there's something
I need to tell you.

482
00:46:49,659 --> 00:46:52,728
Lived here your whole life,
and now she's gotta move...

483
00:46:53,401 --> 00:46:54,263
We're in danger!

484
00:46:54,744 --> 00:46:56,950
Enough! Both of you!

485
00:46:57,428 --> 00:46:58,963
They died, and we lived.

486
00:46:59,442 --> 00:47:00,689
Get over it.

487
00:47:01,169 --> 00:47:02,704
I will not let
this plane crash...

488
00:47:03,279 --> 00:47:05,102
be the most important thing
in my life!

489
00:47:05,581 --> 00:47:06,635
God!

490
00:47:07,115 --> 00:47:09,128
I'm moving on, Carter...

491
00:47:09,609 --> 00:47:11,240
and if you want
to waste your life...

492
00:47:11,719 --> 00:47:14,787
beating the shit out of Alex
every time you see him...

493
00:47:15,266 --> 00:47:17,280
then you can just
drop fucking dead!

494
00:47:44,422 --> 00:47:45,670
Is Alex there?

495
00:47:46,247 --> 00:47:48,260
Hi, Clear.
Yeah, just a minute.

496
00:47:49,507 --> 00:47:51,903
It's Clear again.
You want to talk to her?

497
00:47:56,124 --> 00:47:58,330
Clear, he's in the shower.

498
00:47:58,906 --> 00:47:59,866
Can I get him to call you back?

499
00:48:00,344 --> 00:48:01,783
Sure. Bye.

500
00:48:02,744 --> 00:48:04,949
She's concerned about you.

501
00:48:05,430 --> 00:48:06,771
I'm concerned about you.

502
00:48:07,250 --> 00:48:08,496
Why won't you talk to her?

503
00:48:08,975 --> 00:48:10,894
Or to me?

504
00:48:13,868 --> 00:48:16,073
Dad, you and Mom have
both been a big help...

505
00:48:16,553 --> 00:48:17,800
but there's something
I need to understand...

506
00:48:18,279 --> 00:48:19,527
before I can talk.

507
00:48:19,911 --> 00:48:21,062
To anyone.

508
00:48:23,744 --> 00:48:25,087
The National
Transportation Safety Board...

509
00:48:25,567 --> 00:48:28,062
has a new theory tonight
on the cause of the explosion...

510
00:48:28,541 --> 00:48:30,076
of Volee Air flight 180.

511
00:48:30,652 --> 00:48:32,762
Officials believe deterioration
of silicone insulation...

512
00:48:33,241 --> 00:48:35,255
on an electrical connector
to the scavenger pump...

513
00:48:35,829 --> 00:48:37,846
may have leaked
combustible fluids.

514
00:48:38,420 --> 00:48:39,189
That's Tod's seat.

515
00:48:39,668 --> 00:48:41,105
A spark beneath the coach cabin
in the fuselage...

516
00:48:41,585 --> 00:48:43,600
may have ignited the fuel line,
proceeding to the fuel pump...

517
00:48:44,079 --> 00:48:46,479
which would have set off
the catastrophic explosion.

518
00:49:16,783 --> 00:49:18,126
The path of the explosion.

519
00:49:18,702 --> 00:49:22,731
First was Tod,
then it was Terry.

520
00:49:23,306 --> 00:49:25,320
They're dying in the order
they would've died.

521
00:49:25,798 --> 00:49:27,429
That's death's design.

522
00:49:28,005 --> 00:49:30,115
Ms Lewton's next.

523
00:49:32,896 --> 00:49:35,678
Whenever I can fall asleep,
I see Terry Chaney...

524
00:49:36,158 --> 00:49:38,555
and in the day,
out of nowhere...

525
00:49:39,131 --> 00:49:40,473
I hear the sound of
my own voice in my head...

526
00:49:40,954 --> 00:49:42,488
saying to Larry Murnau...

527
00:49:43,063 --> 00:49:44,406
"No. You know
the whole French thing.

528
00:49:44,983 --> 00:49:45,846
"Get on the plane."

529
00:49:46,421 --> 00:49:48,724
Everything reminds me
of that day.

530
00:49:50,736 --> 00:49:52,941
Right. Yeah.

531
00:49:53,421 --> 00:49:54,285
I'm hoping a change will help.

532
00:49:54,669 --> 00:49:56,587
It's just that...

533
00:49:57,066 --> 00:49:59,848
I lived here
my whole life, you know?

534
00:50:00,423 --> 00:50:02,054
And everywhere I looked
were great memories.

535
00:50:02,533 --> 00:50:07,902
And now all I see
is Larry and those kids.

536
00:50:08,574 --> 00:50:11,452
Just looking out
my own front yard...

537
00:50:12,027 --> 00:50:13,850
makes me feel nothing but fear.

538
00:50:20,180 --> 00:50:22,386
Laura, I'm going to have
to call you back.

539
00:50:22,962 --> 00:50:24,112
Right.

540
00:50:33,223 --> 00:50:35,142
This is Valerie Lewton.
I need Agent Schreck.

541
00:51:00,556 --> 00:51:01,421
What are you doing here?

542
00:51:01,805 --> 00:51:04,202
I was just...

543
00:51:04,778 --> 00:51:07,175
checking the air in the tire
to make sure it's safe.

544
00:51:08,516 --> 00:51:09,667
Get in the car.

545
00:51:20,986 --> 00:51:22,904
Put on your seat belt.

546
00:51:44,677 --> 00:51:46,786
I believe that...

547
00:51:48,224 --> 00:51:50,046
that Ms Lewton is next.

548
00:51:51,004 --> 00:51:52,155
Next?

549
00:51:53,978 --> 00:51:55,513
Yeah, next, because...

550
00:51:55,898 --> 00:51:58,391
there's this pattern
that's occurring.

551
00:51:58,870 --> 00:52:00,212
Right. A pattern.

552
00:52:00,788 --> 00:52:02,131
You noticed that, too?

553
00:52:18,148 --> 00:52:19,300
Mom's favorite.

554
00:52:41,357 --> 00:52:42,796
So what about this pattern?

555
00:52:43,275 --> 00:52:44,907
Did you see it
in one of your visions?

556
00:52:45,481 --> 00:52:47,593
Did you catch it
in some TV static?

557
00:52:50,373 --> 00:52:52,867
I never asked for
what happened on the plane.

558
00:52:53,443 --> 00:52:55,936
You guys are going to
sit there and make fun of me.

559
00:52:56,416 --> 00:52:57,087
Whatever. It's fine.

560
00:52:57,471 --> 00:53:00,348
But I saved six lives
on that plane.

561
00:53:00,923 --> 00:53:02,747
Six lives, and everybody
in my entire school...

562
00:53:03,227 --> 00:53:03,993
thinks I'm a freak.

563
00:53:04,471 --> 00:53:07,926
I'm not suffering
from post-traumatic stress.

564
00:53:08,501 --> 00:53:11,188
I don't have
a narcissistic deity complex.

565
00:53:11,666 --> 00:53:14,447
I'm not going Dahmer
on you guys.

566
00:53:15,023 --> 00:53:16,652
This just is.

567
00:53:17,131 --> 00:53:19,339
There is a pattern for us all.

568
00:53:19,914 --> 00:53:21,641
There is a pattern for you.

569
00:53:22,119 --> 00:53:24,230
There is a design for everyone,
and I don't know how yet...

570
00:53:24,804 --> 00:53:26,243
but I'm going to break
this one.

571
00:54:44,411 --> 00:54:45,848
You came to our attention...

572
00:54:46,233 --> 00:54:48,342
because you were under suspicion
in this plane explosion.

573
00:54:48,918 --> 00:54:50,643
Now...

574
00:54:51,123 --> 00:54:52,466
I know you didn't
blow up the plane.

575
00:54:52,946 --> 00:54:55,249
We were ready to move on...

576
00:54:55,728 --> 00:54:57,453
but then the other survivors
started to die.

577
00:54:57,933 --> 00:54:59,275
First, your friend Tod...

578
00:54:59,754 --> 00:55:01,865
then Terry Chaney,
while you were at the scene.

579
00:55:02,440 --> 00:55:04,167
And tonight, we pick you up
at Val Lewton's house.

580
00:55:04,647 --> 00:55:06,852
Nobody has control
over life and death...

581
00:55:07,427 --> 00:55:10,402
unless they are taking lives
and causing death.

582
00:55:10,978 --> 00:55:13,087
Can you promise me
no one else is going to die?

583
00:55:13,662 --> 00:55:15,387
No, I can't.

584
00:55:15,962 --> 00:55:17,400
As long as I'm in here,
it's out of my control.

585
00:55:17,880 --> 00:55:18,937
I'm sorry.

586
00:55:26,512 --> 00:55:28,624
Go ahead.
Get out of here.

587
00:55:29,774 --> 00:55:30,830
Thank you.

588
00:55:39,175 --> 00:55:40,804
That kid gives me the creeps.

589
00:55:41,283 --> 00:55:42,913
What do we do?
We got nothing.

590
00:55:43,394 --> 00:55:44,833
That's not
what I'm talking about.

591
00:55:45,313 --> 00:55:47,327
There were
a couple of moments...

592
00:55:49,435 --> 00:55:50,873
where I almost believed him.

593
00:55:52,504 --> 00:55:54,231
Don't take this
the wrong way...

594
00:55:54,710 --> 00:55:56,917
but sometimes
you give me the creeps.

595
00:56:13,701 --> 00:56:15,810
You've got to stop this.
It's a stupid mug.

596
00:56:16,290 --> 00:56:17,440
You're out of here.

597
00:56:17,920 --> 00:56:19,071
Pretty soon you'll be gone.

598
00:56:19,551 --> 00:56:20,606
You'll be gone.

599
00:58:41,687 --> 00:58:44,565
All right,
just stay still, OK?

600
00:58:52,910 --> 00:58:54,253
Oh, God, Ms Lewton.

601
00:59:30,699 --> 00:59:31,752
Dude.

602
00:59:34,245 --> 00:59:35,686
I don't know where he is.

603
00:59:36,741 --> 00:59:37,412
He's not talking to me.

604
00:59:37,700 --> 00:59:38,659
Why?

605
00:59:39,234 --> 00:59:40,864
Because I didn't believe him.

606
00:59:41,344 --> 00:59:43,069
If he does contact you,
I think it would be...

607
00:59:43,645 --> 00:59:45,660
in the best interest of
your safety if you called us.

608
00:59:46,236 --> 00:59:48,441
Now, here's my card.

609
00:59:48,920 --> 00:59:50,359
Toll-free number.

610
00:59:54,580 --> 00:59:55,922
Interesting work.

611
01:00:06,951 --> 01:00:08,102
Carter, you dick!

612
01:00:24,693 --> 01:00:26,132
What are you doing?

613
01:00:28,915 --> 01:00:30,545
Terry should be on this wall.

614
01:00:33,039 --> 01:00:34,477
Shit.

615
01:00:35,916 --> 01:00:37,068
Why'd you want us
to meet you here?

616
01:00:37,547 --> 01:00:41,191
They're watching me
to see if I go to Alex.

617
01:00:41,863 --> 01:00:43,397
That's why you're taking me.

618
01:00:43,878 --> 01:00:45,028
Why would I want to see him?

619
01:00:46,467 --> 01:00:48,672
Because he knows
which one of us is next.

620
01:01:01,907 --> 01:01:04,593
Drive the speed limit, OK?

621
01:01:05,168 --> 01:01:07,471
And don't pass on the right.

622
01:01:08,046 --> 01:01:10,253
Billy, wait a minute.
I'm getting a vision.

623
01:01:12,267 --> 01:01:13,322
You're the next one, man.

624
01:01:13,801 --> 01:01:15,046
Man, why'd you say that?

625
01:01:15,526 --> 01:01:18,020
Because if you say another word,
I'll fucking kill you.

626
01:01:37,873 --> 01:01:40,176
He could be anywhere from here
to a mile down the road...

627
01:01:40,655 --> 01:01:43,053
so you guys start that way
and meet back in the middle.

628
01:01:43,724 --> 01:01:45,355
It'll take half the time.

629
01:02:15,087 --> 01:02:16,332
Do you think
they're still up there?

630
01:02:19,019 --> 01:02:21,705
Somehow, flight 180,
are they still in flight?

631
01:02:22,280 --> 01:02:25,253
Somewhere... are they safe?

632
01:02:28,707 --> 01:02:31,869
When I was a kid,
like six or seven...

633
01:02:32,349 --> 01:02:34,460
I used to worry so much
about my parents dying.

634
01:02:34,939 --> 01:02:37,243
Lying awake at night,
just worrying.

635
01:02:37,721 --> 01:02:39,638
Most kids do, I guess.

636
01:02:40,118 --> 01:02:42,706
Most kids never have it happen.

637
01:02:47,503 --> 01:02:48,270
When I was ten...

638
01:02:48,846 --> 01:02:50,764
my dad went into a 7-Eleven
for cigarettes.

639
01:02:51,244 --> 01:02:53,260
He heard someone say,
"Don't turn around."

640
01:02:53,739 --> 01:02:56,997
On reflex, or thinking
it was a friend joking, he did.

641
01:02:58,436 --> 01:02:59,875
A guy blew his head off.

642
01:03:01,506 --> 01:03:03,137
Life became shit after that.

643
01:03:04,192 --> 01:03:06,109
My mom couldn't deal anymore.

644
01:03:07,547 --> 01:03:09,947
She married this asshole
who my mom with my real dad...

645
01:03:10,521 --> 01:03:12,536
would have crossed the street
to avoid this guy.

646
01:03:13,879 --> 01:03:14,837
He didn't really want a kid...

647
01:03:15,221 --> 01:03:17,427
so my mom didn't, either,
anymore.

648
01:03:19,346 --> 01:03:22,894
If that was the design
for my father and family...

649
01:03:23,565 --> 01:03:25,292
then fuck death!

650
01:03:26,443 --> 01:03:30,281
God, I have thought a lot
about that somewhere.

651
01:03:30,760 --> 01:03:32,101
It exists, that place...

652
01:03:32,580 --> 01:03:33,922
where my dad is safe.

653
01:03:34,403 --> 01:03:37,569
Where he had a full pack of
cigarettes and kept driving.

654
01:03:38,816 --> 01:03:40,636
Where me and my mom and my dad
are still together...

655
01:03:41,307 --> 01:03:43,610
and have no idea
about this life here.

656
01:03:46,296 --> 01:03:49,171
Where our friends
are still in the sky.

657
01:03:49,651 --> 01:03:52,721
Where everyone
gets a second chance.

658
01:03:54,064 --> 01:03:56,271
Alex, we can't give up.

659
01:04:01,067 --> 01:04:03,368
I can't go home,
because after Mrs. Lewton...

660
01:04:03,944 --> 01:04:04,806
they're looking for me.

661
01:04:05,286 --> 01:04:06,916
We're taking you
to my dad's cabin.

662
01:04:07,491 --> 01:04:08,547
It's a couple of miles
from my house.

663
01:04:09,026 --> 01:04:11,712
Browning,
you fucking warlock...

664
01:04:12,287 --> 01:04:13,246
did you hear about
Ms Lewton or what?

665
01:04:13,725 --> 01:04:15,452
Why do you think I was hiding?

666
01:04:16,027 --> 01:04:19,097
Billy told the FBI he saw you
running from her house.

667
01:04:19,673 --> 01:04:20,728
I was running 'cause
they blame me for everything.

668
01:04:21,208 --> 01:04:22,452
They blame me
for Tod, for her...

669
01:04:23,028 --> 01:04:23,891
for the fucking plane crash.

670
01:04:24,370 --> 01:04:26,577
The fire caramelized her blood.

671
01:04:27,057 --> 01:04:29,742
Your shoe prints were in it,
your fingerprints on the knives.

672
01:04:30,318 --> 01:04:31,085
I told you they were not...

673
01:04:31,565 --> 01:04:33,004
I'm not talking about
if you did it.

674
01:04:33,579 --> 01:04:35,786
Or even if
you knew she was dead.

675
01:04:36,362 --> 01:04:38,951
Did you know she was going
to be next before she was?

676
01:04:45,664 --> 01:04:46,911
Yeah, I did.

677
01:04:47,965 --> 01:04:49,308
I knew.

678
01:04:49,883 --> 01:04:52,857
All right, out of us...

679
01:04:53,529 --> 01:04:54,583
who's next to see it?

680
01:04:55,063 --> 01:04:56,309
Please...

681
01:04:56,789 --> 01:04:58,515
tell me I'm going to see
the Jets win a Super Bowl.

682
01:04:58,994 --> 01:05:02,065
It's me, right?
That's why you're not saying.

683
01:05:04,366 --> 01:05:07,147
I should have felt up
Tammy in the pool that time.

684
01:05:07,818 --> 01:05:08,778
What are you whining about?
I'm next!

685
01:05:09,259 --> 01:05:11,752
He didn't say anything.
Just drive.

686
01:05:12,231 --> 01:05:14,532
You have a responsibility
to tell me.

687
01:05:15,108 --> 01:05:17,504
Think it's easier to know?

688
01:05:19,615 --> 01:05:21,341
It's not.
It's fucking harder.

689
01:05:21,918 --> 01:05:24,507
You get off
having control over me?

690
01:05:24,986 --> 01:05:26,041
Let me decide
how to deal with it.

691
01:05:26,522 --> 01:05:28,727
It doesn't matter who's next...

692
01:05:29,302 --> 01:05:31,506
'cause we're
all on the same list.

693
01:05:33,521 --> 01:05:34,961
Fuck.

694
01:05:35,440 --> 01:05:36,495
Really?

695
01:05:43,304 --> 01:05:44,264
Then why bother?

696
01:05:44,744 --> 01:05:45,703
What's the fucking point?

697
01:05:46,182 --> 01:05:47,717
Me and Terry'll be back
together on the other side...

698
01:05:48,100 --> 01:05:49,252
so why wait any longer?

699
01:05:50,978 --> 01:05:52,705
What are you doing?

700
01:05:54,910 --> 01:05:56,157
Carter, slow down.

701
01:05:56,636 --> 01:05:58,554
- Slow down, Carter!
- Fuck you!

702
01:06:00,953 --> 01:06:01,721
Knock it off!

703
01:06:02,199 --> 01:06:03,639
May as well go out
on my own free will!

704
01:06:04,118 --> 01:06:05,556
Not with us
in the fucking car!

705
01:06:12,461 --> 01:06:13,899
Jesus, Carter!

706
01:06:15,242 --> 01:06:15,914
Carter, stop the car!

707
01:06:16,489 --> 01:06:18,121
What's the fucking worry
if it ain't your time?

708
01:06:18,504 --> 01:06:19,942
I can get nailed
running this next red light.

709
01:06:20,422 --> 01:06:21,286
You all wouldn't get shit.

710
01:06:21,669 --> 01:06:22,914
It doesn't work that way!

711
01:06:32,027 --> 01:06:34,231
I fucking hated French class!

712
01:06:35,671 --> 01:06:37,206
- Get control of yourself.
- That's what I'm doing!

713
01:06:37,686 --> 01:06:39,891
I know what you're doing,
and it's all right to be scared.

714
01:06:40,466 --> 01:06:42,289
I'm not afraid.
I decide when it's time.

715
01:06:42,768 --> 01:06:45,263
I control my life.
I control my death.

716
01:06:45,742 --> 01:06:47,660
You don't have to prove
how big your balls are!

717
01:06:51,975 --> 01:06:53,224
Stop driving
like such an asshole.

718
01:06:53,799 --> 01:06:54,950
What are you doing?

719
01:06:56,675 --> 01:06:57,825
Jesus Christ!

720
01:06:58,402 --> 01:06:59,936
Put your hands
back on the wheel!

721
01:07:00,510 --> 01:07:01,951
- Carter, stop!
- Back on the wheel!

722
01:07:04,253 --> 01:07:05,498
Shit.

723
01:07:08,088 --> 01:07:09,145
Carter, easy.

724
01:07:09,719 --> 01:07:12,117
I am so close to puking
right now.

725
01:07:14,705 --> 01:07:16,814
We're afraid, too,
but we're not quitting.

726
01:07:17,294 --> 01:07:19,310
Stop it and stop
this fucking car!

727
01:07:20,652 --> 01:07:23,338
Damn it, I want you
to stop this car right now!

728
01:07:42,712 --> 01:07:43,671
- Move it.
- Open the door, Billy.

729
01:07:44,247 --> 01:07:45,494
Move the fucking car!

730
01:07:45,878 --> 01:07:48,658
- Open the fucking door.
- Move the car.

731
01:07:49,999 --> 01:07:52,878
- Can't you open the door?
- Open the fucking door.

732
01:07:54,509 --> 01:07:56,331
- Open it!
- Billy, open the door!

733
01:08:01,032 --> 01:08:03,908
Carter, listen to me.
Don't do this.

734
01:08:08,222 --> 01:08:11,101
God damn it!
Get out of the fucking car!

735
01:08:12,252 --> 01:08:13,978
Carter, listen to me!

736
01:08:14,456 --> 01:08:15,416
This isn't the way!

737
01:08:15,798 --> 01:08:17,429
It's not the way!
Get out of the car!

738
01:08:17,814 --> 01:08:19,157
It's coming!

739
01:08:24,335 --> 01:08:25,391
It ain't my time.

740
01:08:36,134 --> 01:08:36,903
Shit.

741
01:08:37,381 --> 01:08:38,820
Listen to me!
This isn't the way.

742
01:08:39,201 --> 01:08:40,449
Get out of the car!

743
01:08:43,326 --> 01:08:45,820
Come on! Get out!

744
01:08:53,110 --> 01:08:54,260
I can't get out!

745
01:09:05,672 --> 01:09:07,111
Man, he really is next.

746
01:09:08,933 --> 01:09:10,948
Get out!

747
01:09:11,428 --> 01:09:12,579
Fuck!

748
01:09:31,568 --> 01:09:32,623
Oh, God!

749
01:09:34,444 --> 01:09:35,980
I saw it! I saw the belt!

750
01:09:36,459 --> 01:09:37,131
You scared now?

751
01:09:37,706 --> 01:09:38,666
It broke!

752
01:09:39,049 --> 01:09:40,200
Bullshit!

753
01:09:40,680 --> 01:09:41,447
He saved your life again.

754
01:09:41,831 --> 01:09:42,886
That's right!

755
01:09:43,367 --> 01:09:44,422
You're next, Carter!

756
01:09:44,997 --> 01:09:46,627
I'm staying the fuck
away from you!

757
01:09:47,203 --> 01:09:49,119
- Shut up, Billy!
- We don't need this shit now.

758
01:09:49,597 --> 01:09:50,941
I don't need it ever!

759
01:09:51,324 --> 01:09:52,381
Get away from him! He's next!

760
01:09:52,859 --> 01:09:54,011
Fuck you, Billy!
I'm not dead!

761
01:09:54,491 --> 01:09:55,834
You will be.

762
01:09:56,409 --> 01:09:58,039
You're dead...

763
01:09:59,380 --> 01:10:00,533
and you ain't taking me
with you!

764
01:10:04,753 --> 01:10:06,863
- God, no!
- Oh, shit!

765
01:10:15,782 --> 01:10:17,413
Jesus fucking Christ!

766
01:10:19,141 --> 01:10:19,908
You should have been next.

767
01:10:20,484 --> 01:10:22,497
You were after Lewton.
That's the plan!

768
01:10:22,975 --> 01:10:24,796
That's the design!
You should be fucking dead!

769
01:10:25,277 --> 01:10:25,948
You're the devil!

770
01:10:26,524 --> 01:10:28,539
I saw the seat belt.
I knew it would rip!

771
01:10:29,019 --> 01:10:30,456
That's how I saved him.

772
01:10:30,936 --> 01:10:32,182
Just like the explosion.
I saw it!

773
01:10:32,662 --> 01:10:34,389
- The police are coming.
- Fuck! I saved him!

774
01:10:34,965 --> 01:10:36,308
Because I saved him,
it skipped Carter...

775
01:10:36,786 --> 01:10:38,991
and went to the next person
in the path of the explosion!

776
01:10:39,566 --> 01:10:40,813
I have to see it.

777
01:10:41,390 --> 01:10:42,252
Then I intervene...

778
01:10:42,828 --> 01:10:44,651
and if I intervene,
I cheat the design.

779
01:10:45,226 --> 01:10:47,048
Intervene? Are you God now?

780
01:10:47,624 --> 01:10:49,351
God's not afraid to die.

781
01:10:49,925 --> 01:10:51,555
Gods don't die! We do!

782
01:10:52,037 --> 01:10:54,723
Baby, you are losing it.
The police will be here.

783
01:10:55,202 --> 01:10:56,544
We have to get you
back to my cabin.

784
01:10:57,023 --> 01:10:58,462
You can hide out
and get your head together.

785
01:10:58,942 --> 01:11:00,574
After Billy, I'm next.

786
01:11:01,053 --> 01:11:02,108
I'm next.

787
01:11:03,641 --> 01:11:04,409
Then me.

788
01:11:04,791 --> 01:11:07,381
Listen, I'm not going
to let it happen.

789
01:11:07,957 --> 01:11:08,819
Listen to me.

790
01:11:09,301 --> 01:11:12,179
Why don't you two
get the fuck out of here?

791
01:14:00,115 --> 01:14:01,841
Rusted.

792
01:14:02,319 --> 01:14:03,471
Tetanus.

793
01:14:05,195 --> 01:14:06,250
Nice one.

794
01:14:07,977 --> 01:14:09,129
I overlooked it.

795
01:14:11,431 --> 01:14:13,925
You tried to capitalize,
but I caught you.

796
01:14:14,405 --> 01:14:15,554
You fuck!

797
01:14:16,993 --> 01:14:18,145
I can beat you.

798
01:14:19,680 --> 01:14:20,927
Maybe not forever...

799
01:14:21,406 --> 01:14:23,995
but I got this cabin
rigged to beat you now!

800
01:15:14,443 --> 01:15:15,689
She was there a minute ago.

801
01:15:16,745 --> 01:15:17,609
What are you talking about?

802
01:15:18,089 --> 01:15:19,910
I'm not turning him in.

803
01:15:20,965 --> 01:15:22,692
It's too dangerous for him
out there alone.

804
01:15:25,377 --> 01:15:26,625
I go with you.

805
01:15:27,585 --> 01:15:28,735
You can't.

806
01:15:31,419 --> 01:15:32,953
Just tell us where he is.

807
01:15:33,433 --> 01:15:34,775
Wait at home.
I give you my word...

808
01:15:35,256 --> 01:15:37,749
we will bring him in safely
in protective custody.

809
01:16:07,481 --> 01:16:08,537
I never moved.

810
01:16:10,456 --> 01:16:12,277
Christa asked me to change,
but I never moved.

811
01:16:12,756 --> 01:16:14,770
I would have been up
next to Tod.

812
01:16:16,305 --> 01:16:17,840
Fuck!
Why didn't I remember that?

813
01:16:18,416 --> 01:16:20,430
I never moved. Clear's seat
was in front of mine.

814
01:16:36,734 --> 01:16:37,982
She's next.

815
01:17:20,756 --> 01:17:22,769
Get in the car!
We're going around!

816
01:18:28,276 --> 01:18:30,385
- There!
- Come on, let's go!

817
01:18:36,524 --> 01:18:38,250
Alex, we're trying to help you.

818
01:18:41,031 --> 01:18:42,854
Prince! Get away from it!

819
01:18:54,845 --> 01:18:56,186
Hold on!

820
01:19:26,109 --> 01:19:27,165
Run!

821
01:20:11,475 --> 01:20:12,338
We lost him.

822
01:20:12,816 --> 01:20:14,829
From the direction, there's only
one place he's heading.

823
01:20:15,405 --> 01:20:16,748
No shit.

824
01:22:01,673 --> 01:22:02,921
Oh, my God!

825
01:22:08,579 --> 01:22:09,922
Don't move! Stay there!

826
01:22:12,127 --> 01:22:13,278
Don't move!

827
01:22:13,662 --> 01:22:16,443
Don't touch anything.
You're grounded by the tires.

828
01:22:44,832 --> 01:22:46,463
The car's going to explode!

829
01:22:51,451 --> 01:22:53,273
I can only hold on for so long.
You know what to do.

830
01:22:53,945 --> 01:22:54,999
Don't!

831
01:22:55,383 --> 01:22:57,397
When I do this, it'll skip you,
and it'll all be over.

832
01:22:57,876 --> 01:22:59,315
It's the only way
we can cheat death again.

833
01:22:59,892 --> 01:23:00,946
Don't!

834
01:23:01,522 --> 01:23:03,248
I am not going to let it
beat us both.

835
01:23:03,822 --> 01:23:04,972
You know what to do!

836
01:23:07,179 --> 01:23:08,906
I'll always be with you.

837
01:23:12,934 --> 01:23:13,988
Don't!

838
01:23:19,263 --> 01:23:20,799
Get away from there!

839
01:23:45,927 --> 01:23:47,748
Damn it!
You can't do this to me.

840
01:23:48,899 --> 01:23:50,529
Come on, baby. Come on.

841
01:23:54,846 --> 01:23:56,381
Shit, he's not breathing.

842
01:23:56,861 --> 01:23:58,587
Hold on, Alex. Come on.
Stay with me.

843
01:24:34,170 --> 01:24:35,224
Well, we made it.

844
01:24:35,800 --> 01:24:37,430
Paris.
I can't believe it.

845
01:24:38,005 --> 01:24:39,636
I can't believe
we got on a plane again.

846
01:24:39,923 --> 01:24:41,074
Know what I'm saying?

847
01:25:09,177 --> 01:25:10,615
It's weird being here, huh?

848
01:25:11,190 --> 01:25:12,435
We finally completed a puzzle.

849
01:25:14,834 --> 01:25:16,177
It's just
there's something missing.

850
01:25:16,561 --> 01:25:17,616
You know?

851
01:25:20,876 --> 01:25:21,931
To Terry.

852
01:25:22,506 --> 01:25:23,176
And to Tod.

853
01:25:23,657 --> 01:25:24,807
To all of our friends
that can't be here.

854
01:25:25,289 --> 01:25:26,439
Cheers.

855
01:25:30,274 --> 01:25:31,330
What?

856
01:25:32,482 --> 01:25:33,729
If you would've told me
six months ago...

857
01:25:34,304 --> 01:25:36,414
the three of us would be
sitting here having a drink...

858
01:25:38,524 --> 01:25:40,347
I don't know.
Sometimes it feels like...

859
01:25:40,921 --> 01:25:43,129
you two are the only ones
who understand.

860
01:25:46,867 --> 01:25:48,689
You were right, Browning.

861
01:25:49,169 --> 01:25:50,511
It did skip us.
There was a design.

862
01:25:52,143 --> 01:25:53,965
We beat it. We won.

863
01:25:55,211 --> 01:25:58,089
The only thing we won
is a chance at a full life.

864
01:25:59,337 --> 01:26:01,254
A chance
that I won't ever waste.

865
01:26:03,938 --> 01:26:06,624
There's just something
I can't figure out.

866
01:26:07,199 --> 01:26:09,406
- About what?
- The design.

867
01:26:09,982 --> 01:26:11,901
Just let it go, all right?

868
01:26:12,476 --> 01:26:14,777
Hear me out, all right?

869
01:26:16,409 --> 01:26:18,326
- Oh, my God.
- Just listen.

870
01:26:18,901 --> 01:26:21,874
The explosion path determined
the order of our deaths.

871
01:26:22,450 --> 01:26:25,041
When I intervened
and saved Carter...

872
01:26:25,520 --> 01:26:28,589
it skipped him and went
to the next person... Billy.

873
01:26:29,164 --> 01:26:30,026
Then it was Clear.

874
01:26:30,506 --> 01:26:31,944
But I intervened and saved her,
so it went to me.

875
01:26:32,521 --> 01:26:35,878
But in my case,
no one intervened, right?

876
01:26:37,030 --> 01:26:38,178
I was thrown
from that power line...

877
01:26:38,659 --> 01:26:39,904
by the explosion, so...

878
01:26:40,479 --> 01:26:42,111
So why did it skip you?

879
01:26:42,494 --> 01:26:44,701
How do we know this isn't
what was meant to happen?

880
01:26:45,372 --> 01:26:47,099
That out of everyone
on flight 180...

881
01:26:47,674 --> 01:26:49,688
you, me, and Carter
were meant to live?

882
01:26:50,840 --> 01:26:52,663
Maybe that was
the design all along.

883
01:26:53,142 --> 01:26:56,401
- Or you could still be next.
- Shut up, Carter.

884
01:26:57,648 --> 01:26:58,895
I didn't make up the rules.

885
01:26:59,375 --> 01:27:01,198
Somebody's got to intervene
before death can skip them.

886
01:27:01,774 --> 01:27:04,266
Alex proved that three times
with the plane, me, and you.

887
01:27:04,938 --> 01:27:06,376
For all I know,
it could circle back...

888
01:27:06,855 --> 01:27:07,911
and get us all again.

889
01:27:08,486 --> 01:27:10,789
But I am the safest fucker
in the world...

890
01:27:11,363 --> 01:27:13,474
because you're still next.

891
01:27:18,366 --> 01:27:20,092
Can we just stop talking
about this?

892
01:27:20,570 --> 01:27:21,914
Let's figure out...

893
01:27:36,779 --> 01:27:38,889
I'll meet you back
at the hotel.

894
01:27:39,464 --> 01:27:40,232
I'll go with you.

895
01:27:40,711 --> 01:27:43,973
No, just stay here,
OK, Clear?

896
01:27:44,549 --> 01:27:45,219
All right?

897
01:27:45,699 --> 01:27:47,808
See? It's true.
I told you you were next, man.

898
01:27:48,287 --> 01:27:50,493
Stop it, Carter.
Alex, wait.

899
01:27:50,974 --> 01:27:52,892
Clear, stay there, OK?

900
01:27:53,468 --> 01:27:54,522
Stay away.

901
01:28:25,502 --> 01:28:28,475
- I told you you were next!
- It skipped me.

902
01:28:29,050 --> 01:28:30,393
So who's next?

5

7

15

16
00:01:56,825 --> 00:01:59,451

17
00:01:59,619 --> 00:02:03,205

18

ANDY: Hey, sport.

20

They're more--

21

Drink as much as you like.

22

MECHANIC:

23

24

25

take a study break.

26

27

28

29

30

31
00:02:51,421 --> 00:02:53,255
Well, great. What did l just miss?

32
00:02:53,756 --> 00:02:56,925
-Can you see, ma'am?
-l'm sorry, not really.

33
00:02:57,093 --> 00:03:00,179
-Would you like me to move down a bit?

34
00:03:00,346 --> 00:03:01,513
-Be glad to.
-Thanks, man.

35
00:03:01,681 --> 00:03:04,683
-Thank you.
-And I thought chivalry was dead.

36
00:03:04,851 --> 00:03:08,770
Heh. That's not true.
I have my cowboy right here.

37
00:03:08,938 --> 00:03:10,898
Get a room, guys.

38
00:03:16,738 --> 00:03:19,448
Hey. Put these in.

39
00:03:19,616 --> 00:03:21,783

-Put these-- Put them in.

40
00:03:21,951 --> 00:03:24,036

41

BOY 1 : This tickles.
BOY 2: Aw, Mom.

42
00:03:27,790 --> 00:03:30,584
-No, no, no.
HUNT: She put tampons in their ears.

43
00:03:30,752 --> 00:03:31,919
That's just wrong, man.

44
00:03:32,086 --> 00:03:34,421
I can't see now.

45
00:03:34,589 --> 00:03:36,340
ANDY:
Hey, down in front, asshole.

46

60

61
00:04:59,299 --> 00:05:00,340

62

67
00:05:47,930 --> 00:05:49,264

68

69

00:05:58,775 --> 00:06:00,734

71

75
00:06:50,326 --> 00:06:51,993
Oh.

76
00:06:55,164 --> 00:06:56,832

77
00:06:58,501 --> 00:07:00,252

78
00:07:02,672 --> 00:07:04,131
Come on.

79
00:07:05,758 --> 00:07:08,343
[CROWD SCREAMlNG]

80

MAN:
Move!

81
00:07:19,856 --> 00:07:22,441
Go. Get the fuck out of here.

82
00:07:26,028 --> 00:07:27,487
-Come on.
SAMANTHA: Boys, wait!

83
00:07:27,655 --> 00:07:29,030

84

Mom!

85
00:07:34,036 --> 00:07:35,537

86
00:07:35,705 --> 00:07:37,372
[SCREAMS]

87
00:07:46,716 --> 00:07:48,717
[YELLING]

88

Hey! Hey! There's an exit this way!

89
00:07:53,890 --> 00:07:55,056
Come on, move.

90
00:07:58,144 --> 00:07:59,478
[GRUNTS]

91

-Hunt!
-Janet!

92
00:08:14,911 --> 00:08:18,663
-Let's move. Come on.
HUNT: There's an exit over here. Come on.

93
00:08:20,625 --> 00:08:22,709
Come on, come on! This way! Run!

94
00:08:22,877 --> 00:08:23,960
JANET: Lori!
HUNT: Move!

95
00:08:24,128 --> 00:08:25,170
[JANET YELLS]

96
00:08:25,338 --> 00:08:26,838
[RUMBLING ABOVE]

97
00:08:33,137 --> 00:08:35,263
NICK: Lori. Lori, come on, let's go!
Get out of here!

98
00:08:35,431 --> 00:08:37,599
Move! Push through!

99
00:08:42,772 --> 00:08:44,439
Come on!

100
00:08:52,907 --> 00:08:56,117
Ow. Hey. What the hell?

101
00:08:56,285 --> 00:08:57,536
Are you okay?

102
00:08:58,496 --> 00:09:00,747
Nick, is there something wrong?

103
00:09:01,916 --> 00:09:03,625
Oh, my God, is that straight-up Jack?

104
00:09:04,126 --> 00:09:06,253
-No, no, it's his--
-His buddy Jim.

105
00:09:07,255 --> 00:09:10,799
You have been in my stash. You snake.

106
00:09:10,967 --> 00:09:13,468
A cowboy in a hat. He sits here.

107
00:09:13,636 --> 00:09:16,221
COWBOY:
Excuse me. Pardon me.

108
00:09:16,389 --> 00:09:18,181
Oh, ha, ha.

109
00:09:18,349 --> 00:09:20,016
Cowboy.

110
00:09:21,227 --> 00:09:23,937
Tampons. She gets tampons.
She puts them in her kids' ears.

111
00:09:24,105 --> 00:09:25,981
-Who?
-Her, right there.

112
00:09:26,148 --> 00:09:28,400
Hey. Put these in your ears.

113
00:09:28,568 --> 00:09:31,152
BOY 1 : Oh, Mom, not tampons.
-Put them in your ear.

114
00:09:31,320 --> 00:09:33,321
Hey, how did you know
she was gonna do that?

115
00:09:33,489 --> 00:09:35,282
That's a lot of tampons for one woman.

116
00:09:36,325 --> 00:09:40,829
LORI:
